      #!/usr/bin/env python3
      """Render D-0060 2D source-density PNG and PDF maps."""

      from __future__ import annotations

      import json
      from pathlib import Path

      import matplotlib

      matplotlib.use("Agg")
      import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
      import matplotlib.tri as mtri
      import numpy as np
      import pandas as pd
      from matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages
      from matplotlib.colors import LinearSegmentedColormap, LogNorm
      from file_integrity import sha256
      from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
      from matplotlib.patches import Polygon as PolygonPatch


      ANALYSIS = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
      REPO = ANALYSIS.parents[1]
      D0042 = ANALYSIS.parent / "D-0042-hierarchical-debris-drift"
      D0043 = ANALYSIS.parent / "D-0043-generative-debris-drift"
      D0055 = ANALYSIS.parent / "D-0055-bayesian-debris-integration"
      LIGHT_GREYS = LinearSegmentedColormap.from_list(
          "d0060_light_greys", ["#fbfbfb", "#eeeeee", "#d4d4d4", "#a8a8a8"]
      )
      HDR_STYLE = {
          0.50: ("#222222", 1.8),
          0.75: ("#555555", 1.4),
          0.95: ("#888888", 1.1),
      }


      PUBLISHED_CLASS_STYLE = {
          "peer_reviewed_debris_drift": ("#2563eb", "o", "-"),
          "official_debris_drift": ("#15803d", "s", "-"),
          "report_debris_drift": ("#0891b2", "D", "--"),
          "image_conditioned_drift": ("#9333ea", "^", ":"),
          "multi_evidence_compound": ("#ea580c", "P", "-."),
      }
      EXCLUDED_PUBLISHED_COMPARATOR_IDS = {"ugib_2020_drift"}

      CHART_LAYOUT_ROWS: list[dict[str, object]] = []
      MINIMUM_LAYOUT_CLEARANCE_PIXELS = 4.0


      def validate_layout(figure: plt.Figure, stem: Path) -> None:
          """Fail when a publication key or footnote overlaps a data axis."""
          figure.canvas.draw()
          renderer = figure.canvas.get_renderer()
          data_axes = [
              axis for axis in figure.axes
              if axis.get_xlabel() == "Longitude (degrees E)"
          ]
          if not data_axes or len(figure.legends) != 1:
              raise AssertionError("Chart layout audit requires data axes and one figure legend")
          legend_box = figure.legends[0].get_window_extent(renderer)
          axis_boxes = [axis.get_window_extent(renderer) for axis in data_axes]
          if any(legend_box.overlaps(axis_box) for axis_box in axis_boxes):
              raise AssertionError(f"Figure legend overlaps data in {stem.name}")
          notes = [
              artist for artist in figure.texts
              if artist.get_text().startswith("[")
          ]
          if len(notes) != 1:
              raise AssertionError(f"Expected one audit footnote in {stem.name}")
          note_box = notes[0].get_window_extent(renderer)
          if any(note_box.overlaps(axis_box) for axis_box in axis_boxes):
              raise AssertionError(f"Figure footnote overlaps data in {stem.name}")
          if note_box.overlaps(legend_box):
              raise AssertionError(f"Figure footnote overlaps legend in {stem.name}")
          legend_to_data_gap = float(
              min(axis_box.y0 for axis_box in axis_boxes) - legend_box.y1
          )
          note_to_legend_gap = float(legend_box.y0 - note_box.y1)
          if min(legend_to_data_gap, note_to_legend_gap) < MINIMUM_LAYOUT_CLEARANCE_PIXELS:
              raise AssertionError(
                  f"Figure legend or footnote has less than "
                  f"{MINIMUM_LAYOUT_CLEARANCE_PIXELS:g}px clearance in {stem.name}"
              )
          CHART_LAYOUT_ROWS.append(
              {
                  "figure_stem": stem.name,
                  "data_axis_count": len(data_axes),
                  "legend_to_data_vertical_gap_pixels": legend_to_data_gap,
                  "note_to_legend_vertical_gap_pixels": note_to_legend_gap,
                  "legend_bbox_pixels": [float(value) for value in legend_box.bounds],
                  "note_bbox_pixels": [float(value) for value in note_box.bounds],
                  "passes": True,
              }
          )


      def save_both(figure: plt.Figure, stem: Path) -> None:
          validate_layout(figure, stem)
          figure.savefig(stem.with_suffix(".png"), dpi=220, bbox_inches="tight")
          with PdfPages(stem.with_suffix(".pdf")) as pdf:
              pdf.savefig(figure, bbox_inches="tight")
          plt.close(figure)


      def iter_polygons(geometry: dict[str, object]):
          if geometry.get("type") == "Polygon":
              yield geometry["coordinates"]
          elif geometry.get("type") == "MultiPolygon":
              yield from geometry["coordinates"]


      def draw_land(axis: plt.Axes) -> None:
          path = D0043 / "inputs" / "natural_earth_v5.1.2_50m_land.geojson"
          payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          for feature in payload["features"]:
              for polygon in iter_polygons(feature["geometry"]):
                  exterior = np.asarray(polygon[0], dtype=float)
                  if (
                      exterior[:, 0].max() < 50
                      or exterior[:, 0].min() > 122
                      or exterior[:, 1].max() < -50
                      or exterior[:, 1].min() > -14
                  ):
                      continue
                  axis.add_patch(
                      PolygonPatch(
                          exterior,
                          facecolor="#f7f4ee",
                          edgecolor="#777777",
                          linewidth=0.45,
                          zorder=3,
                      )
                  )


      def load_arc() -> np.ndarray:
          payload = json.loads(
              (D0042 / "inputs" / "seventh_arc_fl400.geojson").read_text(
                  encoding="utf-8"
              )
          )
          geometry = payload["features"][0]["geometry"]
          return np.asarray(geometry["coordinates"], dtype=float)


      def structured_triangulation(frame: pd.DataFrame) -> mtri.Triangulation:
          ordered = frame.sort_values(["arc_bin_index", "cross_arc_offset_nm"])
          arc_count = ordered["arc_bin_index"].nunique()
          cross_count = ordered["cross_arc_offset_nm"].nunique()
          if len(ordered) != arc_count * cross_count:
              raise ValueError("Surface is not a complete structured grid")
          triangles: list[tuple[int, int, int]] = []
          for arc_index in range(arc_count - 1):
              for cross_index in range(cross_count - 1):
                  lower_left = arc_index * cross_count + cross_index
                  lower_right = lower_left + 1
                  upper_left = lower_left + cross_count
                  upper_right = upper_left + 1
                  triangles.append((lower_left, lower_right, upper_right))
                  triangles.append((lower_left, upper_right, upper_left))
          return mtri.Triangulation(
              ordered["source_longitude"].to_numpy(float),
              ordered["source_latitude"].to_numpy(float),
              np.asarray(triangles, dtype=int),
          )


      def highest_density_threshold(
          density: np.ndarray, probability_mass: np.ndarray, target: float
      ) -> float:
          order = np.argsort(density)[::-1]
          cumulative = np.cumsum(probability_mass[order])
          index = min(int(np.searchsorted(cumulative, target, side="left")), len(order) - 1)
          return float(density[order[index]])


      def contour_legend() -> list[Line2D]:
          handles = [
              Line2D([0], [0], color="#111111", linestyle="--", label="FL400 seventh arc"),
              Line2D([0], [0], color="#4b7396", linestyle=":", label="cross-arc source limits"),
          ]
          for probability, (color, width) in HDR_STYLE.items():
              handles.append(
                  Line2D(
                      [0],
                      [0],
                      color=color,
                      linewidth=width,
                      label=f"{int(probability * 100)}% highest-density region",
                  )
              )
          handles.append(
              Line2D(
                  [0],
                  [0],
                  marker="*",
                  color="none",
                  markerfacecolor="#f4c542",
                  markeredgecolor="#111111",
                  markersize=11,
                  label="2D density mode",
              )
          )
          return handles


      def draw_density(
          axis: plt.Axes,
          frame: pd.DataFrame,
          *,
          mass_column: str,
          title: str,
      ) -> None:
          ordered = frame.sort_values(["arc_bin_index", "cross_arc_offset_nm"]).copy()
          mass = ordered[mass_column].to_numpy(float)
          measure = ordered["uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2"].to_numpy(float)
          density = mass / measure
          maximum = float(density.max())
          if maximum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maximum):
              raise ValueError("Surface has no positive finite density")
          visual = np.maximum(density, maximum * 1e-4)
          triangulation = structured_triangulation(ordered)
          fill = axis.tricontourf(
              triangulation,
              visual,
              levels=np.geomspace(maximum * 1e-4, maximum, 13),
              cmap=LIGHT_GREYS,
              norm=LogNorm(vmin=maximum * 1e-4, vmax=maximum),
              zorder=1,
          )
          for probability, (color, width) in HDR_STYLE.items():
              threshold = highest_density_threshold(density, mass, probability)
              if density.min() < threshold < density.max():
                  contour = axis.tricontour(
                      triangulation,
                      density,
                      levels=[threshold],
                      colors=[color],
                      linewidths=[width],
                      zorder=5,
                  )
          draw_land(axis)
          arc = load_arc()
          axis.plot(
              arc[:, 0],
              arc[:, 1],
              color="#111111",
              linewidth=1.15,
              linestyle="--",
              zorder=6,
          )
          for offset in (ordered["cross_arc_offset_nm"].min(), ordered["cross_arc_offset_nm"].max()):
              boundary = ordered[ordered["cross_arc_offset_nm"] == offset]
              axis.plot(
                  boundary["source_longitude"],
                  boundary["source_latitude"],
                  color="#4b7396",
                  linewidth=0.9,
                  linestyle=":",
                  zorder=6,
              )
          peak = ordered.iloc[int(np.argmax(density))]
          axis.scatter(
              [peak["source_longitude"]],
              [peak["source_latitude"]],
              marker="*",
              s=150,
              color="#f4c542",
              edgecolor="#111111",
              linewidth=0.8,
              zorder=7,
          )
          axis.annotate(
              f"mode: {abs(float(peak['arc_latitude'])):.0f}°S, "
              f"{float(peak['cross_arc_offset_nm']):+g} NM",
              (peak["source_longitude"], peak["source_latitude"]),
              xytext=(0, 10),
              textcoords="offset points",
              ha="center",
    ── Ran "base=/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/MH370-legacy-pre-refactor-20260824/analyses/D-0060-cross-arc-debris-drift sed -n '260,740p' \""'$base/src/make_2d_charts.py"' (3s)
      $ "base=/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/MH370-legacy-pre-refactor-20260824/analyses/D-0060-cross-arc-debris-drift
    sed -n '260,740p' \""'$base/src/make_2d_charts.py"'
      linestyle=":",
                  zorder=6,
              )
          peak = ordered.iloc[int(np.argmax(density))]
          axis.scatter(
              [peak["source_longitude"]],
              [peak["source_latitude"]],
              marker="*",
              s=150,
              color="#f4c542",
              edgecolor="#111111",
              linewidth=0.8,
              zorder=7,
          )
          axis.annotate(
              f"mode: {abs(float(peak['arc_latitude'])):.0f}°S, "
              f"{float(peak['cross_arc_offset_nm']):+g} NM",
              (peak["source_longitude"], peak["source_latitude"]),
              xytext=(0, 10),
              textcoords="offset points",
              ha="center",
              va="bottom",
              fontsize=8,
              zorder=8,
              bbox={"facecolor": "white", "edgecolor": "none", "alpha": 0.82, "pad": 1.4},
          )
          # Preserve the common publication frame on the frozen +/-100 NM grid, but
          # enlarge it if the predeclared boundary rule expands the source strip.
          # This keeps every evaluated source cell visible without changing density.
          longitude_minimum = float(ordered["source_longitude"].min())
          longitude_maximum = float(ordered["source_longitude"].max())
          latitude_minimum = float(ordered["source_latitude"].min())
          latitude_maximum = float(ordered["source_latitude"].max())
          axis.set_xlim(min(55.0, longitude_minimum - 1.5), max(118.0, longitude_maximum + 1.5))
          axis.set_ylim(min(-47.0, latitude_minimum - 1.0), max(-17.0, latitude_maximum + 1.0))
          axis.set_aspect(1.0 / np.cos(np.radians(-32.0)))
          axis.set_xlabel("Longitude (degrees E)")
          axis.set_title(title)
          axis.set_facecolor("#eef5f7")
          axis.grid(alpha=0.16, linewidth=0.5)
          colorbar = axis.figure.colorbar(fill, ax=axis, shrink=0.72, pad=0.015)
          ticks = maximum * np.geomspace(1e-4, 1.0, 5)
          colorbar.set_ticks(ticks)
          colorbar.set_ticklabels([f"{value:.1e}" for value in ticks])
          colorbar.set_label(
              "Conditional density per strip-coordinate km² (log scale)", fontsize=8
          )
          colorbar.ax.tick_params(labelsize=7)


      def arc_longitude_at_latitude(arc: np.ndarray, latitude_s: float) -> float:
          order = np.argsort(np.abs(arc[:, 1]))
          return float(
              np.interp(
                  latitude_s,
                  np.abs(arc[order, 1]),
                  arc[order, 0],
              )
          )


      def draw_published_context(
          axis: plt.Axes, comparators: pd.DataFrame
      ) -> list[Line2D]:
          """Draw source-reported locations as context, never as likelihood terms."""
          arc = load_arc()
          handles: list[Line2D] = []
          for row in comparators.itertuples(index=False):
              color, marker, linestyle = PUBLISHED_CLASS_STYLE[row.comparison_class]
              lower = float(row.latitude_min_s)
              upper = float(row.latitude_max_s)
              if np.isfinite(lower) and np.isfinite(upper):
                  mask = (
                      (np.abs(arc[:, 1]) >= lower)
                      & (np.abs(arc[:, 1]) <= upper)
                  )
                  if int(np.count_nonzero(mask)) >= 2:
                      axis.plot(
                          arc[mask, 0],
                          arc[mask, 1],
                          color=color,
                          linewidth=3.2,
                          linestyle=linestyle,
                          alpha=0.88,
                          zorder=9,
                      )
              core_lower = float(row.core_min_s)
              core_upper = float(row.core_max_s)
              if np.isfinite(core_lower) and np.isfinite(core_upper):
                  core = (
                      (np.abs(arc[:, 1]) >= core_lower)
                      & (np.abs(arc[:, 1]) <= core_upper)
                  )
                  if int(np.count_nonzero(core)) >= 2:
                      axis.plot(
                          arc[core, 0],
                          arc[core, 1],
                          color=color,
                          linewidth=6.0,
                          alpha=0.90,
                          zorder=10,
                      )
              for column, point_marker, hollow in (
                  ("reported_peak_s", marker, False),
                  ("reported_reference_s", "d", False),
                  ("secondary_mode_s", "o", True),
                  ("tertiary_mode_s", "o", True),
              ):
                  latitude_s = float(getattr(row, column))
                  if not np.isfinite(latitude_s):
                      continue
                  longitude = float(row.longitude_e)
                  if not np.isfinite(longitude):
                      longitude = arc_longitude_at_latitude(arc, latitude_s)
                  axis.scatter(
                      [longitude],
                      [-latitude_s],
                      marker=point_marker,
                      s=64 if not hollow else 44,
                      facecolors="none" if hollow else color,
                      edgecolors=color,
                      linewidths=1.5,
                      zorder=11,
                  )
              handles.append(
                  Line2D(
                      [0],
                      [0],
                      color=color,
                      marker=marker,
                      linestyle=linestyle,
                      linewidth=2.4,
                      markersize=6,
                      label=str(row.label),
                  )
              )
          return handles


      def published_context_figure(
          frame: pd.DataFrame, comparators: pd.DataFrame, note: str
      ) -> plt.Figure:
          figure, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(13.5, 8.8))
          draw_density(
              axis,
              frame,
              mass_column="uniform_strip_reference_cell_mass",
              title=(
                  "D-0060 debris density with source-reported published location context "
                  "(not a meta-analysis)"
              ),
          )
          axis.set_ylabel("Latitude (degrees)")
          published_handles = draw_published_context(axis, comparators)
          figure.legend(
              handles=contour_legend() + published_handles,
              loc="lower center",
              bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 0.018),
              ncol=4,
              frameon=False,
              fontsize=7.4,
          )
          figure.text(
              0.5,
              0.002,
              note,
              ha="center",
              fontsize=7.5,
          )
          figure.subplots_adjust(bottom=0.29, top=0.92, left=0.06, right=0.97)
          return figure


      def single_figure(
          frame: pd.DataFrame,
          *,
          mass_column: str,
          title: str,
          note: str,
      ) -> plt.Figure:
          figure, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.5, 7.5))
          draw_density(axis, frame, mass_column=mass_column, title=title)
          axis.set_ylabel("Latitude (degrees)")
          figure.legend(
              handles=contour_legend(),
              loc="lower center",
              bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 0.02),
              ncol=3,
              frameon=False,
              fontsize=8,
          )
          figure.text(0.5, 0.002, note, ha="center", fontsize=7.7)
          figure.subplots_adjust(bottom=0.17, top=0.92, left=0.07, right=0.96)
          return figure


      def paired_figure(
          left: pd.DataFrame,
          right: pd.DataFrame,
          *,
          mass_column: str,
          left_title: str,
          right_title: str,
          suptitle: str,
          note: str,
      ) -> plt.Figure:
          figure, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(16.5, 7.1), sharex=True, sharey=True)
          draw_density(axes[0], left, mass_column=mass_column, title=left_title)
          draw_density(axes[1], right, mass_column=mass_column, title=right_title)
          axes[0].set_ylabel("Latitude (degrees)")
          figure.suptitle(suptitle, fontsize=14)
          figure.legend(
              handles=contour_legend(),
              loc="lower center",
              bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 0.025),
              ncol=3,
              frameon=False,
              fontsize=8,
          )
          figure.text(0.5, 0.005, note, ha="center", fontsize=7.7)
          figure.subplots_adjust(bottom=0.18, top=0.90, left=0.055, right=0.975, wspace=0.08)
          return figure


      def triplet_figure(
          frames: list[pd.DataFrame],
          *,
          mass_column: str,
          titles: list[str],
          suptitle: str,
          note: str,
      ) -> plt.Figure:
          if len(frames) != 3 or len(titles) != 3:
              raise ValueError("Triplet figure requires exactly three frames and titles")
          figure, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(22.0, 6.8), sharex=True, sharey=True)
          for axis, frame, title in zip(axes, frames, titles, strict=True):
              draw_density(axis, frame, mass_column=mass_column, title=title)
          axes[0].set_ylabel("Latitude (degrees)")
          figure.suptitle(suptitle, fontsize=14)
          figure.legend(
              handles=contour_legend(),
              loc="lower center",
              bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 0.025),
              ncol=3,
              frameon=False,
              fontsize=8,
          )
          figure.text(0.5, 0.005, note, ha="center", fontsize=7.7)
          figure.subplots_adjust(
              bottom=0.20, top=0.88, left=0.04, right=0.985, wspace=0.08
          )
          return figure


      def main() -> None:
          output = ANALYSIS / "outputs" / "production"
          families = pd.read_csv(output / "final_family_raw_likelihood_surfaces.csv")
          combined = pd.read_csv(output / "final_equal_prior_bma_surfaces.csv")
          admission = json.loads(
              (output / "final_admission_audit.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
          )
          if admission.get("schema") != "d0060-final-admission-audit-v5-log-domain-ultra":
              raise AssertionError("Unexpected final admission audit schema")
          debris_admitted = bool(
              admission[
                  "debris_only_raw_family_likelihood_admitted_for_downstream_flight_prior"
              ]
          )
          isotope_admitted = bool(
              admission[
                  "isotope_augmented_raw_family_likelihood_admitted_for_downstream_flight_prior"
              ]
          )
          debris_variant = "debris_only"
          isotope_variant = "debris_plus_isotope_central"
          note = (
              "[DERIVED, CONDITIONAL] Uniform strip-coordinate reference; no end-of-flight "
              "prior. Fill is clipped only for display at 10⁻⁴ of peak density. "
              f"Admission: debris={'PASS' if debris_admitted else 'FAIL'}; "
              f"isotope={'PASS' if isotope_admitted else 'FAIL / sensitivity only'}."
          )
          combined_debris = combined[combined["likelihood_variant"] == debris_variant]
          combined_isotope = combined[combined["likelihood_variant"] == isotope_variant]
          save_both(
              single_figure(
                  combined_debris,
                  mass_column="uniform_strip_reference_cell_mass",
                  title="Debris only: equal-prior HYCOM–GDP model combination (generalized BMA)",
                  note=note,
              ),
              output / "d0060-bma-debris-only-2d-density",
          )
          comparator_source = D0055 / "inputs" / "published_comparators.csv"
          all_comparators = pd.read_csv(comparator_source)
          missing_exclusions = EXCLUDED_PUBLISHED_COMPARATOR_IDS - set(
              all_comparators["comparator_id"].astype(str)
          )
          if missing_exclusions:
              raise AssertionError(
                  f"Requested comparator exclusions are absent: {sorted(missing_exclusions)}"
              )
          comparators = all_comparators[
              ~all_comparators["comparator_id"].isin(EXCLUDED_PUBLISHED_COMPARATOR_IDS)
          ].copy()
          pleiades_conditioned = comparators["comparator_id"].eq("griffin_csiro_2017")
          if int(pleiades_conditioned.sum()) != 1:
              raise AssertionError("Expected one Griffin/CSIRO Pleiades-conditioned comparator")
          comparators.loc[pleiades_conditioned, "label"] = (
              "Griffin/CSIRO 2017 (Pleiades-conditioned)"
          )
          visible_labels = comparators["label"].astype(str)
          if visible_labels.str.contains("godfrey|wspr|kadri", case=False, regex=True).any():
              raise AssertionError("A Godfrey/WSPR/Kadri label remains in the chart comparator set")
          comparator_path = output / "published_context_comparators_used.csv"
          comparators.to_csv(comparator_path, index=False)
          comparator_audit = {
              "schema": "d0060-published-context-chart-audit-v1",
              "source_table": str(comparator_source.relative_to(REPO)),
              "source_table_sha256": sha256(comparator_source),
              "excluded_comparator_ids": sorted(EXCLUDED_PUBLISHED_COMPARATOR_IDS),
              "visible_comparator_ids": comparators["comparator_id"].astype(str).tolist(),
              "godfrey_or_wspr_label_visible": False,
              "used_table_sha256": sha256(comparator_path),
              "kadri_label_visible": False,
              "pleiades_condition_explicit_in_griffin_label": True,
              "published_outputs_are_context_not_pooled_likelihood_terms": True,
          }
          (output / "published_context_chart_audit.json").write_text(
              json.dumps(comparator_audit, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
          )
          context_note = (
              "[DERIVED/SOURCE COMPARISON] Density is the conditional D-0060 debris "
              "model average. Published ranges and markers are author outputs with "
              "different evidence and estimands; they are not pooled likelihood terms."
          )
          save_both(
              published_context_figure(
                  combined_debris,
                  comparators,
                  context_note,
              ),
              output / "d0060-bma-debris-published-context-2d-density",
          )
          save_both(
              single_figure(
                  combined_isotope,
                  mass_column="uniform_strip_reference_cell_mass",
                  title="Debris + A2-G1 isotope: equal-prior HYCOM–GDP model combination (generalized BMA)",
                  note=note,
              ),
              output / "d0060-bma-debris-plus-isotope-2d-density",
          )
          save_both(
              paired_figure(
                  combined_debris,
                  combined_isotope,
                  mass_column="uniform_strip_reference_cell_mass",
                  left_title="Debris only",
                  right_title="Debris + A2-G1 isotope",
                  suptitle="D-0060 two-dimensional source-density comparison",
                  note=note,
              ),
              output / "d0060-bma-debris-vs-isotope-2d-density",
          )
          sensitivity_variants = [
              "debris_plus_isotope_short_memory_tight",
              "debris_plus_isotope_central",
              "debris_plus_isotope_long_memory_wide",
          ]
          sensitivity_frames = [
              combined[combined["likelihood_variant"] == variant]
              for variant in sensitivity_variants
          ]
          save_both(
              triplet_figure(
                  sensitivity_frames,
                  mass_column="uniform_strip_reference_cell_mass",
                  titles=[
                      "Short-memory / tight covariance",
                      "Central covariance",
                      "Long-memory / wide covariance",
                  ],
                  suptitle="D-0060 A2-G1 isotope covariance sensitivity",
                  note=note,
              ),
              output / "d0060-bma-isotope-covariance-sensitivity-2d-density",
          )
          for variant, suffix, label in (
              (debris_variant, "debris-only", "Debris only"),
              (isotope_variant, "debris-plus-isotope", "Debris + A2-G1 isotope"),
          ):
              hycom = families[
                  (families["family"] == "hycom")
                  & (families["likelihood_variant"] == variant)
              ]
              gdp = families[
                  (families["family"] == "gdp")
                  & (families["likelihood_variant"] == variant)
              ]
              save_both(
                  paired_figure(
                      hycom,
                      gdp,
                      mass_column="uniform_strip_reference_conditional_cell_mass",
                      left_title="HYCOM family conditional",
                      right_title="GDP family conditional",
                      suptitle=f"D-0060 {label}: current-family comparison",
                      note=note,
                  ),
                  output / f"d0060-family-{suffix}-2d-density",
              )
          layout_audit = {
              "schema": "d0060-chart-layout-audit-v1",
              "figure_count": len(CHART_LAYOUT_ROWS),
              "minimum_required_clearance_pixels": MINIMUM_LAYOUT_CLEARANCE_PIXELS,
              "all_legends_and_notes_outside_data_axes": True,
              "figures": CHART_LAYOUT_ROWS,
          }
          (output / "chart_layout_audit.json").write_text(
              json.dumps(layout_audit, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
          )
          print("Wrote D-0060 core and published-context 2D maps in PNG and PDF formats")


      if __name__ == "__main__":
          main()

      ## Debris evidence and timing

      The inherited public inventory contains 42 report rows. D-0060 uses the 36 dated
      rows common to both current families: 3 confirmed, 17 likely-or-higher, 7 possible,
      and 9 unconfirmed reports. They form 26 dated site-time events across 17 site
      clusters and are assigned to four object classes: one complete flaperon, 17
      low-exposure exterior reports, four
      high-windage interior reports, and 14 unknown-mixture reports. The allocated
      generalized-likelihood exponents sum to 12.69375; that is a weighting total, not
      an estimated count of independent aircraft parts.

      The five unknown-date rows remain in the inventory but do not receive invented
      dates. The unofficial August 2020 Jeffreys Bay candidate is outside the common
      HYCOM/GDP support ending 30 October 2018 and is not used.
      A refreshed public search on 24 August 2026 found two later unidentified
      aircraft-like candidates (Thailand 2022 and Durban 2025) and one unsupported,
      internally conflicting secondary claim about a December 2024 South Africa panel.
      None has a located official B777/9M-MRO examination or sufficient recovery
      metadata, so none enters the likelihood.

      Identity and co-location are represented through predeclared fractional
      report weights and capped event-site exponents;
      the 36 rows are not treated as 36 independent certain aircraft parts.

      A report date is modeled as first discovery/reporting, not as the beaching date.
      Particles can beach and refloat repeatedly. Before the stated day or month interval,
      a survival term represents the event not having been detected earlier; probability
      mass is accumulated only while a particle is beached near the reported location
      during the reported interval. Month-precision dates span the complete calendar month.

      ## Drift, object, coast, detection, and reporting model

      HYCOM daily analysis and the GDP monthly drifter climatology remain distinct
      structural current families. Each uses the same declared release grid, wind, ERA5
      Stokes field, stochastic diffusion, coast mask, beach/refloat hazards, and
      detection/reporting definitions.

      The complete flaperon receives a component-specific, field-test-centred leeway
      distribution. CSIRO tested a genuine B777 flaperon cut down to resemble the
      recovered part `[S-csiro-2017-ocean-drift-part-ii, p. 8; chunk
      S-csiro-2017-ocean-drift-part-ii:p0008:c01]` and reported about `0.10 m s^-1`
      extra speed plus a mean `16°` left-of-wind angle
      `[S-csiro-2017-ocean-drift-part-ii, p. 16; chunks
      S-csiro-2017-ocean-drift-part-ii:p0016:c02–c03]`. The distribution widths are
      declared assumptions, not fitted sampling distributions. Other reports receive
      class-level priors; these are transported assumptions rather than
      component-specific aircraft trials.

      | Object class | Reports | Wave/leeway draw | Reporting probability |
      | --- | ---: | --- | ---: |
      | Complete flaperon | 1 | Stokes scale `N(1,0.15)`; extra `N(0.10,0.03) m s^-1` at `N(16°,8°)` left of wind | 0.95 |
      | Low-exposure exterior | 17 | Stokes `N(1,0.20)`; direct-wind fraction `N(0.0015,0.0015)` | 0.90 |
      | High-windage interior | 4 | Stokes `N(1,0.25)`; direct-wind fraction `N(0.018,0.006)` | 0.60 |
      | Unknown mixture | 14 | Stokes `N(1,0.25)`; 50:50 low/high direct-wind mixture | 0.50 |

      Normal draws are clipped to the frozen physical ranges recorded in
      `analyses/D-0043-generative-debris-drift/inputs/object_class_priors.csv` and the
      executable sampler. The central coast model uses a 15 km coastal zone, 10-day
      beaching and 171-day refloating timescales, four six-hour substeps, `50 m^2 s^-1`
      diffusivity, and a 100 km recovery-location kernel. First-detection mean delays
      are 45 days for targeted searches and 180 days for opportunistic finds. These are
      declared observation-model assumptions, not rates fitted from a complete
      coast-search denominator.

      The exponential state-transition form follows the stochastic beaching and
      resuspension equations in Onink et al.
      `[S-onink-et-al-2021-global-plastic-coastal-trapping, p. 4; chunks
      S-onink-et-al-2021-global-plastic-coastal-trapping:p0004:c02–c04]`.
      Transferring plastic-debris ranges to aircraft debris, choosing the central
      timescales, and assigning detection delays and report probabilities remain
      D-0043/D-0060 assumptions. Controlled shoreline experiments establish that
      detectability varies with distance, observers, item properties, and substrate,
      but do not supply an MH370 ascertainment denominator
      `[S-burgess-et-al-2024-shoreline-detection, p. 1; chunk
      S-burgess-et-al-2024-shoreline-detection:p0001:c02]`.

      ## Computation and alpha-free rare events

      The ordinary production target is

      ```text
      16,384 particles × 4 classes × 25 arc bins × 9 offsets × 2 families
      = 29,491,200 central trajectories.
      ```

      Nested 8,192-particle prefixes are retained for convergence. Matched fixed-block
      random streams couple offsets within each family/class without coupling HYCOM and
      coverage_ledger_recent

      def logsumexp_weighted(
          weights: np.ndarray, log_values: np.ndarray
      ) -> np.ndarray:
          log_weights = np.full(weights.shape, -np.inf, dtype=float)
          np.log(weights, out=log_weights, where=weights > 0.0)
          terms = log_weights + log_values
          maximum = np.max(terms, axis=1)

      def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
          digest = hashlib.sha256()
          with path.open("rb") as handle:
              for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
                  digest.update(chunk)
          return digest.hexdigest()


      def truth(values: pd.Series) -> np.ndarray:
          mapped = values.astype(str).str.lower().map({"true": True, "false": False})
          if mapped.isna().any():
              raise AssertionError("Boolean audit column contains a non-Boolean value")
          return mapped.to_numpy(bool)


      def png_dimensions(path: Path) -> tuple[int, int]:
          header = path.read_bytes()[:24]
          if len(header) != 24 or header[:8] != b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n":
              raise AssertionError(f"Invalid PNG signature: {path}")
          if header[12:16] != b"IHDR":
              raise AssertionError(f"PNG lacks an IHDR header: {path}")
          return struct.unpack(">II", header[16:24])


      def pdf_page_count(path: Path) -> int:
          payload = path.read_bytes()
          if not payload.startswith(b"%PDF-"):
              raise AssertionError(f"Invalid PDF signature: {path}")
          return len(re.findall(rb"/Type\s*/Page(?!s)\b", payload))


      def append_problem(problems: list[str], condition: bool, message: str) -> None:
          if not condition:
              problems.append(message)

      #!/usr/bin/env python3
      """Isotope-aware weighted ensemble for one flaperon source cell.

      The estimator jointly targets the Reunion recovery and the 49-sample A2-G1
      temperature mark. Resampling is stratified by recovery distance, beach state,
      and the central partial isotope likelihood. The resampling rule changes
      variance, not expectation because each occupied stratum retains its total
      probability weight. All Kalman likelihood states follow the same ancestry map
      as the drift particles.

      Paths that score the recovery and subsequently leave the model domain retain
      their terminal arrival and marked-density contributions before they are removed
      from the active ensemble.
      """

      from __future__ import annotations

      import argparse
      import csv
      import json
      import math
      import sys
      from datetime import timedelta
      from pathlib import Path

      import numpy as np
      import pandas as pd

      from file_integrity import sha256


      REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
      D0045_SRC = REPO / "analyses" / "D-0045-rare-event-debris-likelihood" / "src"
      sys.path.insert(0, str(D0045_SRC))
      from run_target import (  # noqa: E402
          COAST_CASES,
          RELEASE_DATE,
          build_fields,
          d42core,
          load_target,
          sample_object_parameters,
          score_target_day,
          stable_seed,
          step,
      )
      from weighted_ensemble_core import (  # noqa: E402
          distance_beach_strata,
          stratified_resample,
      )

      from isotope_likelihood import load_default_bank  # noqa: E402


      FLAPERON_RECORD_ID = "IMG-001"


      def load_source_cell(path: Path, source_cell_id: str) -> dict[str, object]:
          with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
              selected = [
                  row
                  for row in csv.DictReader(handle)
                  if row["source_cell_id"] == source_cell_id
              ]
          if len(selected) != 1:
              raise ValueError(f"Expected one source-grid row for {source_cell_id!r}")
          row: dict[str, object] = selected[0]
          for name in (
              "arc_bin_index",
              "arc_latitude",
              "arc_longitude",
              "along_arc_chainage_km_from_20s",
              "cross_arc_offset_nm",
              "source_latitude",
              "source_longitude",
          ):
              row[name] = int(row[name]) if name == "arc_bin_index" else float(row[name])
          return row


      def rank_quantile_labels(values: np.ndarray, bins: int) -> np.ndarray:
          """Deterministic equal-count rank strata; non-finite values remain in bin zero."""
          values = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
          if bins < 1:
              raise ValueError("At least one likelihood stratum is required")
          labels = np.zeros(len(values), dtype=int)
          finite = np.flatnonzero(np.isfinite(values))
          if bins == 1 or len(finite) < 2 or np.all(values[finite] == values[finite[0]]):
              return labels
          ordered = finite[np.argsort(values[finite], kind="stable")]
          labels[ordered] = np.minimum(
              bins - 1, np.arange(len(ordered), dtype=int) * bins // len(ordered)
          )
          return labels


      def log_weighted_moments(
          weights: np.ndarray, values: np.ndarray, log_mark: np.ndarray
      ) -> tuple[float, float]:
          """Log sum and log squared-sum of weighted marked contributions."""
          contribution = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float) * np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
          log_mark = np.asarray(log_mark, dtype=float)
          positive = (contribution > 0.0) & np.isfinite(log_mark)
          if not np.any(positive):
              return -math.inf, -math.inf
          terms = np.log(contribution[positive]) + log_mark[positive]
          maximum = float(np.max(terms))
          log_sum = maximum + math.log(float(np.exp(terms - maximum).sum()))
          log_square_sum = 2.0 * maximum + math.log(
              float(np.exp(2.0 * (terms - maximum)).sum())
          )
          return log_sum, log_square_sum


      def kish_ess_from_moments(total: float, squared_sum: float) -> float:
          return total * total / squared_sum if squared_sum > 0.0 else 0.0


      def log_moment_ess(log_sum: float, log_square_sum: float) -> float:
          if not np.isfinite(log_sum) or not np.isfinite(log_square_sum):
              return 0.0
          return float(np.exp(2.0 * log_sum - log_square_sum))


      def add_log_marked_contributions_by_lineage(
          accumulator: np.ndarray,
          lineage: np.ndarray,
          weights: np.ndarray,
          values: np.ndarray,
          log_mark: np.ndarray,
      ) -> None:
          """Accumulate marked weighted contributions by original trajectory lineage."""
          contribution = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float) * np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
          lineage = np.asarray(lineage, dtype=int)
          log_mark = np.asarray(log_mark, dtype=float)
          positive = (contribution > 0.0) & np.isfinite(log_mark)
          if np.any(positive):
              terms = np.log(contribution[positive]) + log_mark[positive]
              np.logaddexp.at(accumulator, lineage[positive], terms)


      def lineage_log_sum(log_contribution_by_lineage: np.ndarray) -> float:
          """Stable sum of positive contributions stored on the log scale."""
          values = np.asarray(log_contribution_by_lineage, dtype=float)
          finite = np.isfinite(values)
          if not np.any(finite):
              return -math.inf
          maximum = float(np.max(values[finite]))
          return maximum + math.log(float(np.exp(values[finite] - maximum).sum()))


      def lineage_log_moment_ess(log_contribution_by_lineage: np.ndarray) -> float:
          """Kish ESS after descendants are conservatively reunited by root lineage."""
          values = np.asarray(log_contribution_by_lineage, dtype=float)
          finite = np.isfinite(values)
          if not np.any(finite):
              return 0.0
          maximum = float(np.max(values[finite]))
          scaled = np.exp(values[finite] - maximum)
          return float(np.square(scaled.sum()) / np.square(scaled).sum())


      def one_replicate(
          *,
          analysis: Path,
          release: tuple[float, float],
          fields,
          particles: int,
          seed: int,
          resampling_days: int,
          boundaries_km: np.ndarray,
          likelihood_bins: int,
      ) -> tuple[list[dict[str, object]], list[dict[str, object]]]:
          target = load_target(analysis, "flaperon", FLAPERON_RECORD_ID)
          current, wind, stokes, coast = fields
          rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
          latitude = np.full(particles, release[0], dtype=float)
          longitude = np.full(particles, release[1], dtype=float)
          beached = np.zeros(particles, dtype=bool)
          lost = np.zeros(particles, dtype=bool)
          parameters = sample_object_parameters("flaperon", (particles,), rng)
          survival = np.ones(particles, dtype=float)
          detection_mass = np.zeros(particles, dtype=float)
          weights = np.full(particles, 1.0 / particles, dtype=float)
          isotope = load_default_bank(analysis, (particles,))
          model_names = tuple(isotope.marginalized_log_likelihoods())
          root_lineage = np.arange(particles, dtype=int)
          terminal_arrival_by_root = np.zeros(particles, dtype=float)
          terminal_log_joint_by_root = {name: np.full(particles, -math.inf) for name in model_names}
          terminal_arrival_probability = 0.0
          terminal_arrival_squared_sum = 0.0
          terminal_scored_contributors = 0
          terminal_log_joint = {name: -math.inf for name in model_names}
          terminal_log_joint_squared_sum = {name: -math.inf for name in model_names}
          diagnostics: list[dict[str, object]] = []
          when = RELEASE_DATE
          day_index = 0
          while True:
              isotope.update_day(when, latitude, longitude)
              score_target_day(
                  when,
                  latitude,
                  longitude,
                  beached,
                  target,
                  survival,
                  detection_mass,
                  "central",
              )
              if when == target["date_end_value"]:
                  break
              for _ in range(4):
                  step(
                      latitude,
                      longitude,
                      beached,
                      lost,
                      when,
                      current,
                      wind,
                      stokes,
                      coast,
                      parameters,
                      rng,
                      COAST_CASES["central"],
                  )
              when += timedelta(days=1)
              day_index += 1
              if resampling_days > 0 and day_index % resampling_days == 0:
                  if np.any(lost):
                      lost_arrival = weights[lost] * detection_mass[lost]
                      np.add.at(
                          terminal_arrival_by_root,
                          root_lineage[lost],
                          lost_arrival,
                      )
                      terminal_arrival_probability += float(lost_arrival.sum())
                      terminal_arrival_squared_sum += float(
                          np.square(lost_arrival).sum()
                      )
                      terminal_scored_contributors += int(
                          np.count_nonzero(lost_arrival > 0.0)
                      )
                      if np.any(lost_arrival > 0.0):
                          observed_counts = set(
                              isotope.audit()["measured_samples_per_chronology"].values()
                          )
                          if observed_counts != {49}:
                              raise AssertionError(
                                  "A scored terminal path lacks the complete isotope record"
                              )
                          marks = isotope.marginalized_log_likelihoods()
                          for name in model_names:
                              log_sum, log_square_sum = log_weighted_moments(
                                  weights[lost],
                                  detection_mass[lost],
                                  marks[name][lost],
                              )
                              add_log_marked_contributions_by_lineage(
                                  terminal_log_joint_by_root[name],
                                  root_lineage[lost],
                                  weights[lost],
                                  detection_mass[lost],
                                  marks[name][lost],
                              )
                              terminal_log_joint[name] = float(
                                  np.logaddexp(terminal_log_joint[name], log_sum)
                              )
                              terminal_log_joint_squared_sum[name] = float(
                                  np.logaddexp(
                                      terminal_log_joint_squared_sum[name],
                                      log_square_sum,
                                  )
                              )
                  active = ~lost
                  if not np.any(active):
                      weights = weights[:0]
                      detection_mass = detection_mass[:0]
                      root_lineage = root_lineage[:0]
                      break
                  distance = d42core.haversine_grid_km(
                      latitude[active],
                      longitude[active],
                      float(target["latitude_value"]),
                      float(target["longitude_value"]),
                  )
                  base = distance_beach_strata(distance, beached[active], boundaries_km)
                  partial = isotope.marginalized_log_likelihoods()["central"][active]
                  isotope_label = rank_quantile_labels(partial, likelihood_bins)
                  base_count = 2 * (len(boundaries_km) + 1)
                  labels = base + base_count * isotope_label
                  active_index = np.flatnonzero(active)
                  result = stratified_resample(labels, weights[active], particles, rng)
                  source = active_index[result.selected_indices]
                  root_lineage = root_lineage[source].copy()
                  latitude = latitude[source].copy()
                  longitude = longitude[source].copy()
                  beached = beached[source].copy()
                  lost = np.zeros(particles, dtype=bool)
                  survival = survival[source].copy()
                  detection_mass = detection_mass[source].copy()
                  parameters = {
                      name: values[source].copy() for name, values in parameters.items()
                  }
                  isotope.resample(source)
                  weights = result.weights
                  diagnostics.append(
                      {
                          "day": day_index,
                          "date": when.isoformat(),
                          "occupied_strata": result.occupied_strata,
                          "likelihood_strata_occupied": int(np.unique(isotope_label).size),
                          "effective_sample_size_before": result.effective_sample_size_before,
                          "effective_sample_size_after": result.effective_sample_size_after,
                          "active_probability_weight": float(weights.sum()),
                          "active_root_lineages_after": int(np.unique(root_lineage).size),
                          "terminal_arrival_probability_accumulated": terminal_arrival_probability,
                          "minimum_reunion_distance_km": float(distance.min()),
                      }
                  )

          if len(weights):
              current_arrival_weight = weights * detection_mass
              current_marks = isotope.marginalized_log_likelihoods()
          else:
              current_arrival_weight = np.empty(0, dtype=float)
              current_marks = {
                  name: np.empty(0, dtype=float) for name in model_names
              }
          arrival_probability = (
              terminal_arrival_probability + float(current_arrival_weight.sum())
          )
          arrival_squared_sum = (
              terminal_arrival_squared_sum
              + float(np.square(current_arrival_weight).sum())
          )
          arrival_ess = kish_ess_from_moments(
              arrival_probability, arrival_squared_sum
          )
          arrival_by_root = terminal_arrival_by_root.copy()
          if len(weights):
              np.add.at(arrival_by_root, root_lineage, current_arrival_weight)
          arrival_lineage_total = float(arrival_by_root.sum())
          if not np.isclose(arrival_lineage_total, arrival_probability, rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-15):
              raise AssertionError("Arrival root-lineage total differs from particle total")
          arrival_lineage_ess = kish_ess_from_moments(
              arrival_lineage_total,
              float(np.square(arrival_by_root).sum()),
          )
          if arrival_lineage_ess > arrival_ess + 1e-9:
              raise AssertionError("Arrival root-lineage ESS exceeds particle-weight ESS")
          log_arrival = (
              math.log(arrival_probability) if arrival_probability > 0.0 else -math.inf
          )
          rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
          for name in model_names:
              if len(weights):
                  current_log_sum, current_log_square_sum = log_weighted_moments(
                      weights, detection_mass, current_marks[name]
                  )
              else:
                  current_log_sum, current_log_square_sum = -math.inf, -math.inf
              log_joint = float(
                  np.logaddexp(terminal_log_joint[name], current_log_sum)
              )
              log_joint_square_sum = float(
                  np.logaddexp(
                      terminal_log_joint_squared_sum[name],
                      current_log_square_sum,
                  )
              )
              joint_by_root = terminal_log_joint_by_root[name].copy()
              if len(weights):
                  add_log_marked_contributions_by_lineage(
                      joint_by_root,
                      root_lineage,
                      weights,
                      detection_mass,
                      current_marks[name],
                  )
              lineage_log_joint = lineage_log_sum(joint_by_root)
              both_zero = np.isneginf(lineage_log_joint) and np.isneginf(log_joint)
              if not both_zero and not math.isclose(lineage_log_joint, log_joint, rel_tol=1e-12, abs_tol=1e-10):
                  raise AssertionError("Isotope root-lineage total differs from particle total")
              joint_lineage_ess = lineage_log_moment_ess(joint_by_root)
              joint_particle_ess = log_moment_ess(log_joint, log_joint_square_sum)
              if joint_lineage_ess > joint_particle_ess + 1e-9:
                  raise AssertionError("Isotope root-lineage ESS exceeds particle-weight ESS")
              rows.append(
                  {
                      "isotope_likelihood_model": name,
                      "arrival_probability": arrival_probability,
                      "terminal_arrival_probability": terminal_arrival_probability,
                      "terminal_scored_particle_contributions": terminal_scored_contributors,
                      "log_joint_arrival_isotope_density": log_joint,
                      "log_isotope_density_conditional_on_arrival": (
                          log_joint - log_arrival
                          if np.isfinite(log_joint) and np.isfinite(log_arrival)
                          else math.nan
                      ),
                      "arrival_weight_effective_sample_size": arrival_ess,
                      "arrival_root_lineage_effective_sample_size": arrival_lineage_ess,
                      "arrival_contributing_root_lineages": int(np.count_nonzero(arrival_by_root > 0.0)),
                      "isotope_arrival_root_lineage_effective_sample_size": joint_lineage_ess,
                      "isotope_contributing_root_lineages": int(np.count_nonzero(np.isfinite(joint_by_root))),
                      "isotope_arrival_weight_effective_sample_size": joint_particle_ess,
                  }
              )
          return rows, diagnostics


      def main() -> None:
          parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
          parser.add_argument("--family", choices=("hycom", "gdp"), required=True)
          parser.add_argument("--source-cell-id", required=True)
          parser.add_argument(
              "--stage", choices=("pilot", "production"), default="production"
          )
          parser.add_argument(
              "--output-tier", choices=("screen", "final"), default="final"
          )
          parser.add_argument("--particles", type=int)
          parser.add_argument("--replicates", type=int)
          args = parser.parse_args()
          analysis = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
          config_path = analysis / "inputs" / "config.json"
          source_grid_path = analysis / "inputs" / "source_grid.csv"
          diagnostic_freeze_path = analysis / "inputs" / "isotope_diagnostic_freeze.json"
          config = json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          particles = args.particles or int(
              config["isotope_weighted_particles_per_replicate"]
          )
          replicates = args.replicates or int(config["isotope_weighted_replicates"])
          source = load_source_cell(
              analysis / "inputs" / "source_grid.csv", args.source_cell_id
          )
          allowed_offsets = {float(value) for value in config[f"{args.stage}_offsets_nm"]}
          if float(source["cross_arc_offset_nm"]) not in allowed_offsets:
              raise ValueError("Source cell is not in the requested stage grid")
          physical_fields = build_fields(analysis, args.family)[:4]
          rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
          diagnostics: list[dict[str, object]] = []
          for replicate in range(replicates):
              seed = stable_seed(
                  int(config["isotope_weighted_base_seed"]),
                  "d0060-isotope-weighted-v1",
                  args.family,
                  args.source_cell_id,
                  particles,
                  replicate,
              )
              estimates, history = one_replicate(
                  analysis=analysis,
                  release=(
                      float(source["source_latitude"]),
                      float(source["source_longitude"]),
                  ),
                  fields=physical_fields,
                  particles=particles,
                  seed=seed,
                  resampling_days=int(
                      config["isotope_weighted_resampling_interval_days"]
                  ),
                  boundaries_km=np.asarray(
                      config["rare_event_distance_strata_km"], dtype=float
                  ),
                  likelihood_bins=int(
                      config["isotope_weighted_likelihood_quantile_strata"]
                  ),
              )
              rows.extend(
                  {
                      "schema": "d0060-isotope-weighted-v2",
                      "stage": args.stage,
                      "family": args.family,
                      "source_cell_id": args.source_cell_id,
                      "arc_bin_index": source["arc_bin_index"],
                      "arc_latitude": source["arc_latitude"],
                      "cross_arc_offset_nm": source["cross_arc_offset_nm"],
                      "source_latitude": source["source_latitude"],
                      "source_longitude": source["source_longitude"],
                      "particles_per_replicate": particles,
                      "replicate": replicate,
                      "seed": seed,
                      **estimate,
                  }
                  for estimate in estimates
              )
              diagnostics.extend(
                  {"replicate": replicate, "seed": seed, **item} for item in history
              )
              print(
                  f"{args.family}/{args.source_cell_id} replicate "
                  f"{replicate + 1}/{replicates}",
                  flush=True,
              )
          subdirectory = "isotope_screen" if args.output_tier == "screen" else "isotope_weighted"
          output = analysis / "outputs" / args.stage / subdirectory
          output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
          stem = f"isotope_weighted_{args.family}_{args.source_cell_id}"
          frame = pd.DataFrame(rows)
          frame.to_csv(output / f"{stem}.csv", index=False)
          pd.DataFrame(diagnostics).to_csv(
              output / f"{stem}_resampling.csv", index=False
          )
          summary = {
              "schema": "d0060-isotope-weighted-metadata-v4",
              "stage": args.stage,
              "output_tier": args.output_tier,
              "family": args.family,
              "source_cell_id": args.source_cell_id,
              "particles_per_replicate": particles,
              "replicates": replicates,
              "resampling_interval_days": config[
                  "isotope_weighted_resampling_interval_days"
              ],
              "likelihood_quantile_strata": config[
                  "isotope_weighted_likelihood_quantile_strata"
              ],
              "output_rows": len(frame),
              "alpha_or_pseudocount": 0.0,
              "config_sha256": sha256(config_path),
              "source_grid_sha256": sha256(source_grid_path),
              "isotope_diagnostic_freeze_sha256": sha256(diagnostic_freeze_path),
              "root_lineage_ess_exported": True,
              "particle_weight_ess_retained_as_diagnostic": True,
              "particle_weight_ess_is_also_an_admission_gate": True,
              "lineage_totals_checked_against_particle_totals": True,
              "terminal_contributions_retained_before_lost_paths_are_removed": True,
          }
          (output / f"{stem}.json").write_text(
              json.dumps(summary, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
          )


      if __name__ == "__main__":
          main()
