      #!/usr/bin/env python3
      """Audit sensitivity to the fractional-likelihood learning rate.

      The debris evidence is a predeclared fractional/power likelihood.  This module
      does not select or calibrate a learning rate.  It holds the proper conditional
      isotope density at exponent one and varies only the generalized debris factor,
      then reports how family weights and normalized source surfaces move around the
      primary eta=1 specification.
      """

      from __future__ import annotations

      import hashlib
      import json
      from pathlib import Path

      import numpy as np
      import pandas as pd

      from surface_core import (
          bayesian_model_average_log_likelihood,
          hellinger_distance,
          normalize_log_surface,
          posterior_family_probabilities,
          total_variation,
      )


      FAMILIES = ("hycom", "gdp")


      def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
          digest = hashlib.sha256()
          with path.open("rb") as handle:
              for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
                  digest.update(chunk)
          return digest.hexdigest()


      def array_from_frame(
          frame: pd.DataFrame,
          column: str,
          arc_indices: list[int],
          offsets: list[float],
      ) -> np.ndarray:
          pivot = frame.pivot(
              index="arc_bin_index",
              columns="cross_arc_offset_nm",
              values=column,
          ).reindex(index=arc_indices, columns=offsets)
          if pivot.isna().any().any():
              raise AssertionError(f"Incomplete sensitivity lattice for {column}")
          return pivot.to_numpy(float)


      def surface_summary(
          grid: pd.DataFrame,
          raw: np.ndarray,
          mass: np.ndarray,
          *,
          estimator: str,
          variant: str,
          learning_rate: float,
      ) -> dict[str, object]:
          selected = grid.copy()
          selected["_mass"] = mass.reshape(-1)
          selected["_density"] = (
              selected["_mass"]
              / selected["uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2"]
          )
          if (
              not np.isfinite(selected["_mass"]).all()
              or np.any(selected["_mass"] < 0.0)
              or not np.isclose(selected["_mass"].sum(), 1.0)
          ):
              raise AssertionError("Sensitivity surface is not normalized")
          mode = selected.iloc[int(selected["_density"].to_numpy(float).argmax())]
          along = (
              selected.groupby(
                  ["arc_bin_index", "arc_latitude", "arc_longitude"],
                  as_index=False,
              )
              .agg(
                  marginal_mass=("_mass", "sum"),
                  marginal_quadrature=(
                      "uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2",
                      "sum",
                  ),
              )
          )
          along["marginal_density"] = (
              along["marginal_mass"] / along["marginal_quadrature"]
          )
          along_mode = along.sort_values(
              ["marginal_density", "arc_bin_index"],
              ascending=[False, True],
          ).iloc[0]
          cross = (
              selected.groupby("cross_arc_offset_nm", as_index=False)
              .agg(
                  marginal_mass=("_mass", "sum"),
                  marginal_quadrature=(
                      "uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2",
                      "sum",
                  ),
              )
          )
          cross["marginal_density"] = (
              cross["marginal_mass"] / cross["marginal_quadrature"]
          )
          cross_mode = cross.sort_values(
              ["marginal_density", "cross_arc_offset_nm"],
              ascending=[False, True],
          ).iloc[0]
          arc_limits = (
              int(selected["arc_bin_index"].min()),
              int(selected["arc_bin_index"].max()),
          )
          offset_limits = (
              float(selected["cross_arc_offset_nm"].min()),
              float(selected["cross_arc_offset_nm"].max()),
          )
          along_boundary = selected["arc_bin_index"].isin(arc_limits)
          cross_boundary = (
              np.isclose(selected["cross_arc_offset_nm"], offset_limits[0])
              | np.isclose(selected["cross_arc_offset_nm"], offset_limits[1])
          )
          absolute_latitude = selected["arc_latitude"].abs()
          mode_index = np.unravel_index(int(np.argmax(raw)), raw.shape)
          return {
              "estimator": estimator,
              "likelihood_variant": variant,
              "debris_learning_rate_eta": learning_rate,
              "density_mode_source_cell_id": str(mode["source_cell_id"]),
              "density_mode_arc_latitude_deg": float(mode["arc_latitude"]),
              "density_mode_cross_arc_offset_nm": float(
                  mode["cross_arc_offset_nm"]
              ),
              "density_mode_arc_index": int(mode_index[0]),
              "density_mode_cross_index": int(mode_index[1]),
              "along_arc_marginal_density_mode_latitude_deg": float(
                  along_mode["arc_latitude"]
              ),
              "cross_arc_marginal_density_mode_offset_nm": float(
                  cross_mode["cross_arc_offset_nm"]
              ),
              "along_arc_endpoint_mass": float(
                  selected.loc[along_boundary, "_mass"].sum()
              ),
              "cross_arc_boundary_mass": float(
                  selected.loc[cross_boundary, "_mass"].sum()
              ),
              "mass_20_to_24s": float(
                  selected.loc[absolute_latitude.between(20.0, 24.0), "_mass"].sum()
              ),
              "mass_28_to_44s": float(
                  selected.loc[absolute_latitude.between(28.0, 44.0), "_mass"].sum()
              ),
          }


      def calculate_sensitivity(
          family_frame: pd.DataFrame,
          combined_frame: pd.DataFrame,
          specification: dict[str, object],
      ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict[str, object]]:
          required = {
              "family",
              "likelihood_variant",
              "source_cell_id",
              "arc_bin_index",
              "arc_latitude",
              "arc_longitude",
              "cross_arc_offset_nm",
              "source_latitude",
              "source_longitude",
              "uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2",
              "raw_log_likelihood",
              "uniform_strip_reference_conditional_cell_mass",
          }
          missing = sorted(required - set(family_frame.columns))
          if missing:
              raise AssertionError(f"Family sensitivity input lacks columns: {missing}")
          if set(family_frame["family"].astype(str)) != set(FAMILIES):
              raise AssertionError("Sensitivity requires both current families")
          key = ["family", "likelihood_variant", "source_cell_id"]
          if family_frame.duplicated(key).any():
              raise AssertionError("Family sensitivity key is not unique")
          variants = sorted(family_frame["likelihood_variant"].astype(str).unique())
          if "debris_only" not in variants:
              raise AssertionError("Sensitivity input lacks debris_only")
          learning_rates = sorted(
              {float(value) for value in specification["learning_rates"]}
          )
          reference = float(specification["reference_learning_rate"])
          if (
              not learning_rates
              or any(not np.isfinite(value) or value <= 0.0 for value in learning_rates)
              or reference not in learning_rates
          ):
              raise AssertionError("Learning rates must be finite, positive, and include reference")
          prior_spec = specification["family_prior"]
          if not isinstance(prior_spec, dict):
              raise AssertionError("Family prior specification is malformed")
          prior = np.asarray([float(prior_spec[name]) for name in FAMILIES])
          if np.any(prior <= 0.0) or not np.isclose(prior.sum(), 1.0):
              raise AssertionError("Sensitivity family prior must be positive and sum to one")

          geometry_columns = [
              "source_cell_id",
              "arc_bin_index",
              "arc_latitude",
              "arc_longitude",
              "cross_arc_offset_nm",
              "source_latitude",
              "source_longitude",
              "uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2",
          ]
          grid = (
              family_frame[
                  (family_frame["family"] == FAMILIES[0])
                  & (family_frame["likelihood_variant"] == "debris_only")
              ][geometry_columns]
              .sort_values(["arc_bin_index", "cross_arc_offset_nm"])
              .copy()
          )
          if grid["source_cell_id"].duplicated().any():
              raise AssertionError("Sensitivity geometry key is not unique")
          arc_indices = sorted(int(value) for value in grid["arc_bin_index"].unique())
          offsets = sorted(float(value) for value in grid["cross_arc_offset_nm"].unique())
          if len(grid) != len(arc_indices) * len(offsets):
              raise AssertionError("Sensitivity geometry is not a complete lattice")
          measure = array_from_frame(
              grid,
              "uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2",
              arc_indices,
              offsets,
          )
          if not np.isfinite(measure).all() or np.any(measure <= 0.0):
              raise AssertionError("Sensitivity quadrature must be finite and positive")

          debris_raw: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
          original_raw: dict[tuple[str, str], np.ndarray] = {}
          for family in FAMILIES:
              for variant in variants:
                  selected = family_frame[
                      (family_frame["family"] == family)
                      & (family_frame["likelihood_variant"] == variant)
                  ]
                  raw = array_from_frame(
                      selected,
                      "raw_log_likelihood",
                      arc_indices,
                      offsets,
                  )
                  if not np.isfinite(raw).all():
                      raise AssertionError(
                          f"Sensitivity requires finite raw logs for {family}/{variant}"
                      )
                  original_raw[(family, variant)] = raw
              debris_raw[family] = original_raw[(family, "debris_only")]

          rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
          distributions: dict[tuple[str, str, float], tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]] = {}
          maximum_family_reference_mass_residual = 0.0
          maximum_combined_reference_mass_residual = 0.0
          combined_key = ["likelihood_variant", "source_cell_id"]
          if combined_frame.duplicated(combined_key).any():
              raise AssertionError("Combined sensitivity key is not unique")

          for variant in variants:
              isotope_mark = {
                  family: original_raw[(family, variant)] - debris_raw[family]
                  for family in FAMILIES
              }
              if variant == "debris_only":
                  for family in FAMILIES:
                      if not np.array_equal(isotope_mark[family], np.zeros_like(isotope_mark[family])):
                          raise AssertionError("Debris-only mark decomposition is not zero")
              for eta in learning_rates:
                  adjusted_by_family: list[np.ndarray] = []
                  mass_by_family: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
                  evidence: list[float] = []
                  for family in FAMILIES:
                      adjusted = eta * debris_raw[family] + isotope_mark[family]
                      mass, log_evidence = normalize_log_surface(adjusted, measure)
                      adjusted_by_family.append(adjusted)
                      mass_by_family[family] = mass
                      evidence.append(log_evidence)
                  family_weight = posterior_family_probabilities(
                      np.asarray(evidence, dtype=float), prior
                  )
                  combined_raw = bayesian_model_average_log_likelihood(
                      np.stack(adjusted_by_family, axis=0), prior
                  )
                  combined_mass, _ = normalize_log_surface(combined_raw, measure)
                  for family_index, family in enumerate(FAMILIES):
                      item = surface_summary(
                          grid,
                          adjusted_by_family[family_index],
                          mass_by_family[family],
                          estimator=family,
                          variant=variant,
                          learning_rate=eta,
                      )
                      item["hycom_generalized_family_weight"] = float(family_weight[0])
                      item["gdp_generalized_family_weight"] = float(family_weight[1])
                      rows.append(item)
                      distributions[(variant, family, eta)] = (
                          adjusted_by_family[family_index],
                          mass_by_family[family],
                      )
                  item = surface_summary(
                      grid,
                      combined_raw,
                      combined_mass,
                      estimator="equal_prior_generalized_bma",
                      variant=variant,
                      learning_rate=eta,
                  )
                  item["hycom_generalized_family_weight"] = float(family_weight[0])
                  item["gdp_generalized_family_weight"] = float(family_weight[1])
                  rows.append(item)
                  distributions[(variant, "equal_prior_generalized_bma", eta)] = (
                      combined_raw,
                      combined_mass,
                  )

                  if eta == reference:
                      for family in FAMILIES:
                          selected = family_frame[
                              (family_frame["family"] == family)
                              & (family_frame["likelihood_variant"] == variant)
                          ]
                          exported_mass = array_from_frame(
                              selected,
                              "uniform_strip_reference_conditional_cell_mass",
                              arc_indices,
                              offsets,
                          )
                          maximum_family_reference_mass_residual = max(
                              maximum_family_reference_mass_residual,
                              float(np.max(np.abs(exported_mass - mass_by_family[family]))),
                          )
                      selected_combined = combined_frame[
                          combined_frame["likelihood_variant"] == variant
                      ]
                      exported_combined = array_from_frame(
                          selected_combined,
                          "uniform_strip_reference_cell_mass",
                          arc_indices,
                          offsets,
                      )
                      maximum_combined_reference_mass_residual = max(
                          maximum_combined_reference_mass_residual,
                          float(np.max(np.abs(exported_combined - combined_mass))),
                      )

          frame = pd.DataFrame(rows)
          for index, row in frame.iterrows():
              key = (
                  str(row["likelihood_variant"]),
                  str(row["estimator"]),
                  float(row["debris_learning_rate_eta"]),
              )
              raw, mass = distributions[key]
              reference_raw, reference_mass = distributions[
                  (key[0], key[1], reference)
              ]
              current_mode = np.unravel_index(int(np.argmax(raw)), raw.shape)
              reference_mode = np.unravel_index(
                  int(np.argmax(reference_raw)), reference_raw.shape
              )
              frame.loc[index, "total_variation_from_eta_1"] = total_variation(
                  mass, reference_mass
              )
              frame.loc[index, "hellinger_distance_from_eta_1"] = hellinger_distance(
                  mass, reference_mass
              )
              frame.loc[index, "mode_shift_arc_bins_from_eta_1"] = abs(
                  current_mode[0] - reference_mode[0]
              )
              frame.loc[index, "mode_shift_cross_bins_from_eta_1"] = abs(
                  current_mode[1] - reference_mode[1]
              )
          frame = frame.sort_values(
              ["likelihood_variant", "estimator", "debris_learning_rate_eta"]
          ).reset_index(drop=True)
          if maximum_family_reference_mass_residual > 1e-12:
              raise AssertionError("Eta=1 family sensitivity surface does not reproduce export")
          if maximum_combined_reference_mass_residual > 1e-12:
              raise AssertionError("Eta=1 combined sensitivity surface does not reproduce export")
          audit = {
              "schema": "d0060-generalized-bayes-learning-rate-sensitivity-audit-v1",
              "status": "sensitivity only; not an admission gate",
              "reference_learning_rate": reference,
              "learning_rates": learning_rates,
              "tempered_factor": specification["tempered_factor"],
              "isotope_factor_rule": specification["isotope_factor_rule"],
              "ordinary_posterior_probability_calibration_claimed": False,
              "row_count": len(frame),
              "maximum_eta_1_family_mass_reproduction_residual": maximum_family_reference_mass_residual,
              "maximum_eta_1_combined_mass_reproduction_residual": maximum_combined_reference_mass_residual,
              "maximum_total_variation_from_eta_1": float(
                  frame["total_variation_from_eta_1"].max()
              ),
              "maximum_hellinger_distance_from_eta_1": float(
                  frame["hellinger_distance_from_eta_1"].max()
              ),
              "maximum_axis_mode_shift_bins_from_eta_1": int(
                  frame[
                      [
                          "mode_shift_arc_bins_from_eta_1",
                          "mode_shift_cross_bins_from_eta_1",
                      ]
                  ].to_numpy(float).max()
              ),
              "alpha_or_pseudocount_used": False,
          }
          return frame, audit


      def build(analysis: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, object]:
          analysis = analysis or Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
          output = analysis / "outputs" / "production"
          specification_path = (
              analysis / "inputs" / "generalized_bayes_learning_rate_sensitivity.json"
          )
          family_path = output / "final_family_raw_likelihood_surfaces.csv"
          combined_path = output / "final_equal_prior_bma_surfaces.csv"
          specification = json.loads(specification_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          frame, audit = calculate_sensitivity(
              pd.read_csv(family_path),
              pd.read_csv(combined_path),
              specification,
          )
          table_path = output / "generalized_bayes_learning_rate_sensitivity.csv"
          audit_path = output / "generalized_bayes_learning_rate_sensitivity_audit.json"
          frame.to_csv(table_path, index=False)
          audit.update(
              {
                  "specification_sha256": sha256(specification_path),
                  "final_family_surface_sha256": sha256(family_path),
                  "final_combined_surface_sha256": sha256(combined_path),
                  "output_table_sha256": sha256(table_path),
              }
          )
          audit_path.write_text(
              json.dumps(audit, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
          )
          return {
              "audit": audit,
              "table_file": table_path.name,
              "rows": [
                  {
                      key: (
                          int(value)
                          if isinstance(value, np.integer)
                          else float(value)
                          if isinstance(value, np.floating)
                          else value
                      )
                      for key, value in row.items()
                  }
                  for row in frame.to_dict(orient="records")
              ],
          }


      def main() -> None:
          print(json.dumps(build(), indent=2))


      if __name__ == "__main__":
          main()
      sensitivity_tests
      from __future__ import annotations

      import sys
      import unittest
      from pathlib import Path

      import numpy as np
      import pandas as pd


      SRC = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src"
      sys.path.insert(0, str(SRC))
      from build_generalized_bayes_sensitivity import calculate_sensitivity  # noqa: E402
      from surface_core import (  # noqa: E402
          bayesian_model_average_log_likelihood,
          normalize_log_surface,
      )


      class GeneralizedBayesSensitivityTests(unittest.TestCase):
          def fixture(self) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, dict[str, object]]:
              cells = [
                  {
                      "source_cell_id": "a",
                      "arc_bin_index": 0,
                      "arc_latitude": -20.0,
                      "arc_longitude": 100.0,
                      "cross_arc_offset_nm": -25.0,
                      "source_latitude": -20.0,
                      "source_longitude": 100.0,
                      "uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2": 1.0,
                  },
                  {
                      "source_cell_id": "b",
                      "arc_bin_index": 0,
                      "arc_latitude": -20.0,
                      "arc_longitude": 100.0,
                      "cross_arc_offset_nm": 25.0,
                      "source_latitude": -20.0,
                      "source_longitude": 101.0,
                      "uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2": 1.0,
                  },
                  {
                      "source_cell_id": "c",
                      "arc_bin_index": 1,
                      "arc_latitude": -30.0,
                      "arc_longitude": 90.0,
                      "cross_arc_offset_nm": -25.0,
                      "source_latitude": -30.0,
                      "source_longitude": 90.0,
                      "uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2": 1.0,
                  },
                  {
                      "source_cell_id": "d",
                      "arc_bin_index": 1,
                      "arc_latitude": -30.0,
                      "arc_longitude": 90.0,
                      "cross_arc_offset_nm": 25.0,
                      "source_latitude": -30.0,
                      "source_longitude": 91.0,
                      "uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2": 1.0,
                  },
              ]
              debris = {
                  "hycom": np.array([[-1.0, -7.0], [-20.0, -20.0]]),
                  "gdp": np.array([[-3.0, -2.0], [-20.0, -20.0]]),
              }
              isotope_mark = {
                  "hycom": np.array([[-4.0, 0.0], [-4.0, -4.0]]),
                  "gdp": np.array([[-1.0, -2.0], [-4.0, -4.0]]),
              }
              measure = np.ones((2, 2), dtype=float)
              family_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
              combined_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
              prior = np.array([0.5, 0.5])
              for variant in ("debris_only", "debris_plus_isotope_central"):
                  raw_by_family: list[np.ndarray] = []
                  for family in ("hycom", "gdp"):
                      raw = debris[family].copy()
                      if variant != "debris_only":
                          raw = raw + isotope_mark[family]
                      mass, _ = normalize_log_surface(raw, measure)
                      raw_by_family.append(raw)
                      for index, cell in enumerate(cells):
                          family_rows.append(
                              {
                                  **cell,
                                  "family": family,
                                  "likelihood_variant": variant,
                                  "raw_log_likelihood": float(raw.reshape(-1)[index]),
                                  "uniform_strip_reference_conditional_cell_mass": float(
                                      mass.reshape(-1)[index]
                                  ),
                              }
                          )
                  combined_raw = bayesian_model_average_log_likelihood(
                      np.stack(raw_by_family), prior
                  )
                  combined_mass, _ = normalize_log_surface(combined_raw, measure)
                  for index, cell in enumerate(cells):
                      combined_rows.append(
                          {
                              **cell,
                              "likelihood_variant": variant,
                              "raw_log_likelihood": float(
                                  combined_raw.reshape(-1)[index]
                              ),
                              "uniform_strip_reference_cell_mass": float(
                                  combined_mass.reshape(-1)[index]
                              ),
                          }
                      )
              specification = {
                  "reference_learning_rate": 1.0,
                  "learning_rates": [0.5, 1.0, 2.0],
                  "tempered_factor": "debris generalized log likelihood only",
                  "isotope_factor_rule": "isotope retained at exponent one",
                  "family_prior": {"hycom": 0.5, "gdp": 0.5},
              }
              return (
                  pd.DataFrame(family_rows),
                  pd.DataFrame(combined_rows),
                  specification,
              )

          def test_eta_one_reproduces_export_and_family_weights_sum_to_one(self) -> None:
              rows, audit = calculate_sensitivity(*self.fixture())
              self.assertEqual(len(rows), 18)
              self.assertLess(
                  audit["maximum_eta_1_family_mass_reproduction_residual"], 1e-12
              )
              self.assertLess(
                  audit["maximum_eta_1_combined_mass_reproduction_residual"], 1e-12
              )
              reference = rows[np.isclose(rows["debris_learning_rate_eta"], 1.0)]
              self.assertTrue(
                  np.allclose(reference["total_variation_from_eta_1"], 0.0)
              )
              self.assertTrue(
                  np.allclose(
                      rows["hycom_generalized_family_weight"]
                      + rows["gdp_generalized_family_weight"],
                      1.0,
                  )
              )

          def test_isotope_mark_is_not_tempered_with_fractional_debris_factor(self) -> None:
              rows, _ = calculate_sensitivity(*self.fixture())
              selected = rows[
                  (rows["estimator"] == "hycom")
                  & (rows["likelihood_variant"] == "debris_plus_isotope_central")
              ].set_index("debris_learning_rate_eta")
              self.assertEqual(selected.loc[1.0, "density_mode_source_cell_id"], "a")
              self.assertEqual(selected.loc[0.5, "density_mode_source_cell_id"], "b")
              self.assertGreater(
                  selected.loc[0.5, "total_variation_from_eta_1"], 0.0
              )


      if __name__ == "__main__":
          unittest.main()

      from __future__ import annotations

      def keys(frame: pd.DataFrame) -> set[tuple[str, str, str, str]]:
          return set(
              zip(
                  frame["family"].astype(str),
                  frame["object_class"].astype(str),
                  frame["source_cell_id"].astype(str),
                  frame["record_id"].astype(str),

      def main() -> None:
          analysis = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
          repo = analysis.parents[1]
          inputs = analysis / "inputs"
          output = analysis / "outputs" / "production"
          config_path = inputs / "config.json"
          repair_path = inputs / "log_domain_rare_repair.json"
          selector_audit_path = inputs / "rare_event_hybrid_strong_target_matrix.audit.json"
          strong_target_path = inputs / "rare_event_hybrid_strong_target_matrix.csv"
          guarded_path = output / "rare_log_replay_guarded_family_cells.csv"
          predecessor_quality_path = output / "rare_refinement_final_quality.csv"
          predecessor_audit_path = output / "rare_refinement_final_audit.json"
          predecessor_quality_archive = output / "rare_refinement_linear_predecessor_quality.csv"
          predecessor_audit_archive = output / "rare_refinement_linear_predecessor_audit.json"
          log_target_path = inputs / "rare_refinement_rank5_target_matrix.csv"
          strong_manifest_path = output / "rare_refinement_event_hybrid_strong_matrix_run.json"
          ultra_path = inputs / "ultra_log_rare_refinement.json"
          ultra_selector_audit_path = inputs / "rare_event_hybrid_ultra_target_matrix.audit.json"
          ultra_target_path = inputs / "rare_event_hybrid_ultra_target_matrix.csv"
          ultra_manifest_path = output / "rare_refinement_event_hybrid_ultra_matrix_run.json"
          strong_quality_path = output / "rare_refinement_strong_log_quality.csv"
          required = (
              config_path,
              repair_path,
              selector_audit_path,
              strong_target_path,
              guarded_path,
              predecessor_quality_path,
              predecessor_audit_path,
              log_target_path,
              strong_manifest_path,
              ultra_path,
              ultra_selector_audit_path,
              ultra_target_path,
              ultra_manifest_path,
              strong_quality_path,
          )
          for path in required:
              if not path.exists():
                  raise FileNotFoundError(path)
          repair = json.loads(repair_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          admission = repair["admission"]
          selector_audit = json.loads(selector_audit_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          predecessor_quality_key = str(predecessor_quality_path.relative_to(repo))
          predecessor_expected_hash = selector_audit["input_hashes"].get(
              predecessor_quality_key
          )
          canonical_matches = (
              predecessor_expected_hash == sha256(predecessor_quality_path)
          )
          archive_matches = bool(
              predecessor_quality_archive.exists()
              and predecessor_expected_hash == sha256(predecessor_quality_archive)
          )
          if canonical_matches:
              predecessor_quality_source = predecessor_quality_path
              predecessor_audit_source = predecessor_audit_path
              shutil.copy2(predecessor_quality_source, predecessor_quality_archive)
              shutil.copy2(predecessor_audit_source, predecessor_audit_archive)
          elif archive_matches:
              predecessor_quality_source = predecessor_quality_archive
              predecessor_audit_source = predecessor_audit_archive
              if not predecessor_audit_source.exists():
                  raise FileNotFoundError(predecessor_audit_source)
          else:
              raise AssertionError("No predecessor quality table matches strong selection")
          predecessor_audit = json.loads(
              predecessor_audit_source.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
          )
          if predecessor_audit.get("input_hashes", {}).get(
              predecessor_quality_key
          ) != predecessor_expected_hash:
              raise AssertionError("Predecessor audit does not bind the selected quality table")
          strong_manifest = json.loads(strong_manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          if strong_manifest.get("schema") != "d0060-event-hybrid-strong-log-matrix-v1":
              raise AssertionError("Unexpected strong matrix manifest")
          if strong_manifest.get("alpha_or_pseudocount_used") is not False:
              raise AssertionError("Strong matrix manifest permits an alpha or pseudocount")
          if strong_manifest.get("failed"):
              raise AssertionError("Strong event-log matrix contains failed tasks")
          if strong_manifest.get("log_repair_config_sha256") != sha256(repair_path):
              raise AssertionError("Strong event-log matrix used a different repair specification")
          if strong_manifest.get("target_matrix_sha256") != sha256(strong_target_path):
              raise AssertionError("Strong event-log matrix used a different target matrix")
          if strong_manifest.get("base_config_sha256") != sha256(config_path):
              raise AssertionError("Strong event-log matrix used a different base configuration")
          strong_specification = repair["material_refinement"]
          expected_strong_parameters = {
              "particles_per_replicate": int(
                  strong_specification["particles_per_replicate"]
              ),
              "replicates": int(strong_specification["replicates"]),
              "resampling_interval_days": int(
                  strong_specification["resampling_interval_days"]
              ),
              "fixed_distance_boundaries_km": strong_specification[
                  "fixed_distance_boundaries_km"
              ],
              "within_fixed_bin_rank_strata": int(
                  strong_specification["within_fixed_bin_rank_strata"]
              ),
          }
          for name, expected in expected_strong_parameters.items():
              if strong_manifest.get(name) != expected:
                  raise AssertionError(f"Strong matrix manifest parameter mismatch for {name}")

          ultra = json.loads(ultra_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          ultra_selector_audit = json.loads(ultra_selector_audit_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          ultra_manifest = json.loads(ultra_manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          if ultra.get("schema") != "d0060-ultra-log-rare-refinement-v1":
              raise AssertionError("Unexpected ultra-refinement specification")
          if ultra_selector_audit.get("schema") != "d0060-ultra-log-rare-selection-v1":
              raise AssertionError("Unexpected ultra-selection audit")
          if (
              ultra_selector_audit.get(
                  "location_blind_rule_frozen_before_remaining_strong_outputs"
              )
              is not True
          ):
              raise AssertionError("Ultra selection was not recorded as prospectively frozen")
          trigger = ultra.get("trigger", {})
          if (
              trigger.get("location_blind") is not True
              or trigger.get("map_values_used_to_choose_rule") is not False
          ):
              raise AssertionError("Ultra selection rule is not explicitly location blind")
          ultra_admission = ultra["admission"]
          for name in (
              "minimum_finite_replicates",
              "maximum_replicate_coefficient_of_variation_at_guarded_material_cells",
              "all_final_event_log_probabilities_must_be_finite",
              "alpha_or_pseudocount",
          ):
              if ultra_admission.get(name) != admission.get(name):
                  raise AssertionError(f"Ultra and final admission disagree for {name}")
          if float(ultra_admission["alpha_or_pseudocount"]) != 0.0:
              raise AssertionError("Ultra admission permits an alpha or pseudocount")
          expected_selector_inputs = (
              repair_path,
              ultra_path,
              strong_target_path,
              strong_manifest_path,
          )
          for path in expected_selector_inputs:
              key = str(path.relative_to(repo))
              if ultra_selector_audit.get("input_hashes", {}).get(key) != sha256(path):
                  raise AssertionError(f"Ultra selector input hash mismatch for {path}")
          for path in (strong_quality_path, ultra_target_path):
              key = str(path.relative_to(repo))
              if ultra_selector_audit.get("output_hashes", {}).get(key) != sha256(path):
                  raise AssertionError(f"Ultra selector output hash mismatch for {path}")
          if ultra_manifest.get("schema") != "d0060-event-hybrid-ultra-log-matrix-v1":
              raise AssertionError("Unexpected ultra matrix manifest")
          if ultra_manifest.get("failed"):
              raise AssertionError("Ultra event-log matrix contains failed tasks")
          if ultra_manifest.get("base_config_sha256") != sha256(config_path):
              raise AssertionError("Ultra event-log matrix used a different base configuration")
          if ultra_manifest.get("log_repair_config_sha256") != sha256(repair_path):
              raise AssertionError("Ultra event-log matrix used a different repair specification")
          if ultra_manifest.get("ultra_refinement_config_sha256") != sha256(ultra_path):
              raise AssertionError("Ultra event-log matrix used a different ultra specification")
          if ultra_manifest.get("target_matrix_sha256") != sha256(ultra_target_path):
              raise AssertionError("Ultra event-log matrix used a different target matrix")
          if ultra_manifest.get("alpha_or_pseudocount_used") is not False:
              raise AssertionError("Ultra matrix manifest permits an alpha or pseudocount")
          if ultra_manifest.get("independent_full_replacement") is not True:
              raise AssertionError("Ultra matrix is not an independent full replacement")
          ultra_specification = ultra["ultra_material_refinement"]
          expected_ultra_parameters = {
              "particles_per_replicate": int(
                  ultra_specification["particles_per_replicate"]
              ),
              "replicates": int(ultra_specification["replicates"]),
              "resampling_interval_days": int(
                  ultra_specification["resampling_interval_days"]
              ),
              "fixed_distance_boundaries_km": ultra_specification[
                  "fixed_distance_boundaries_km"
              ],
              "within_fixed_bin_rank_strata": int(
                  ultra_specification["within_fixed_bin_rank_strata"]
              ),
          }
          for name, expected in expected_ultra_parameters.items():
              if ultra_manifest.get(name) != expected:
                  raise AssertionError(f"Ultra matrix manifest parameter mismatch for {name}")

          off_arc = pd.read_csv(inputs / "rare_target_matrix.csv")
          zero = pd.read_csv(inputs / "zero_offset_anchor_matrix.csv")
          base = pd.concat([off_arc, zero], ignore_index=True)
          key_columns = ["family", "object_class", "source_cell_id", "record_id"]
          if base.duplicated(key_columns).any():
              raise AssertionError("Combined rare-event base key is not unique")
          base_keys = keys(base)
          predecessor = pd.read_csv(predecessor_quality_source)
          if predecessor.duplicated(key_columns).any() or keys(predecessor) != keys(base):
              raise AssertionError("Predecessor quality coverage differs from rare targets")
          rank5 = pd.read_csv(log_target_path)
          strong = pd.read_csv(strong_target_path)
          ultra_targets = pd.read_csv(ultra_target_path)
          rank5_keys = keys(rank5)
          strong_keys = keys(strong)
          ultra_keys = keys(ultra_targets)
          if not rank5_keys.issubset(base_keys):
              raise AssertionError("Rank5 targets are not a subset of the rare-event base")
          if not strong_keys.issubset(rank5_keys):
              raise AssertionError("Strong targets are not a subset of rank5 targets")
          if not ultra_keys.issubset(strong_keys):
              raise AssertionError("Ultra targets are not a subset of strong targets")
          if int(ultra_manifest.get("total_task_count", -1)) != len(ultra_targets):
              raise AssertionError("Ultra matrix task count differs from its target matrix")
          if int(
              ultra_selector_audit.get("ultra_selected_event_probability_count", -1)
          ) != len(ultra_targets):
              raise AssertionError("Ultra selector count differs from its target matrix")
          if ultra_targets.duplicated(key_columns).any():
              raise AssertionError("Ultra target key is not unique")
          guarded_frame = pd.read_csv(guarded_path)
          guarded = set(
              zip(
                  guarded_frame["family"].astype(str),
                  guarded_frame["source_cell_id"].astype(str),
              )
          )
          predecessor_by_key = {
              (
                  str(row.family),
                  str(row.object_class),
                  str(row.source_cell_id),
                  str(row.record_id),
              ): row
              for row in predecessor.itertuples(index=False)
          }

          @lru_cache(maxsize=None)
          def rank5_log_table(family: str, object_class: str, source_cell_id: str):
              stem = f"grouped_{family}_{object_class}_{source_cell_id}"
              path = output / "rare_refinement_rank5_log" / f"{stem}.csv"
              if not path.exists():
                  raise FileNotFoundError(path)
              return pd.read_csv(path), path

          @lru_cache(maxsize=None)
          def predecessor_table(path_text: str):
              path = repo / path_text
              if not path.exists():
                  raise FileNotFoundError(path)
              return pd.read_csv(path), path

          @lru_cache(maxsize=None)
          def strong_table(
              family: str, object_class: str, source_cell_id: str, record_id: str
          ):
              stem = event_stem(family, object_class, source_cell_id, record_id)
              path = output / "rare_refinement_event_hybrid_strong" / f"{stem}.csv"
              metadata_path = path.with_suffix(".json")
              if not path.exists() or not metadata_path.exists():
                  raise FileNotFoundError(path)
              metadata = json.loads(metadata_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
              if not (
                  metadata.get("schema") == "d0060-event-hybrid-strong-log-metadata-v1"
                  and metadata.get("family") == family
                  and metadata.get("object_class") == object_class
                  and metadata.get("source_cell_id") == source_cell_id
                  and str(metadata.get("record_id")) == record_id
                  and metadata.get("base_config_sha256") == sha256(config_path)
                  and metadata.get("particles_per_replicate")
                  == expected_strong_parameters["particles_per_replicate"]
                  and metadata.get("replicates") == expected_strong_parameters["replicates"]
                  and metadata.get("resampling_interval_days")
                  == expected_strong_parameters["resampling_interval_days"]
                  and metadata.get("log_repair_config_sha256") == sha256(repair_path)
                  and metadata.get("target_matrix_sha256") == sha256(strong_target_path)
                  and metadata.get("alpha_or_pseudocount") == 0.0
                  and metadata.get("fixed_distance_boundaries_km")
                  == expected_strong_parameters["fixed_distance_boundaries_km"]
                  and metadata.get("within_fixed_bin_rank_strata")
                  == expected_strong_parameters["within_fixed_bin_rank_strata"]
              ):
                  raise AssertionError(f"Strong event metadata mismatch in {metadata_path}")
              return pd.read_csv(path), path

          @lru_cache(maxsize=None)
          def ultra_table(
              family: str, object_class: str, source_cell_id: str, record_id: str
          ):
              stem = ultra_event_stem(family, object_class, source_cell_id, record_id)
              path = output / "rare_refinement_event_hybrid_ultra" / f"{stem}.csv"
              metadata_path = path.with_suffix(".json")
              if not path.exists() or not metadata_path.exists():
                  raise FileNotFoundError(path)
              metadata = json.loads(metadata_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
              if not (
                  metadata.get("schema") == "d0060-event-hybrid-ultra-log-metadata-v1"
                  and metadata.get("family") == family
                  and metadata.get("object_class") == object_class
                  and metadata.get("source_cell_id") == source_cell_id
                  and str(metadata.get("record_id")) == record_id
                  and metadata.get("base_config_sha256") == sha256(config_path)
                  and metadata.get("particles_per_replicate")
                  == expected_ultra_parameters["particles_per_replicate"]
                  and metadata.get("replicates") == expected_ultra_parameters["replicates"]
                  and metadata.get("resampling_interval_days")
                  == expected_ultra_parameters["resampling_interval_days"]
                  and metadata.get("log_repair_config_sha256") == sha256(repair_path)
                  and metadata.get("ultra_refinement_config_sha256") == sha256(ultra_path)
                  and metadata.get("target_matrix_sha256") == sha256(ultra_target_path)
                  and metadata.get("independent_full_replacement") is True
                  and metadata.get("alpha_or_pseudocount") == 0.0
                  and metadata.get("fixed_distance_boundaries_km")
                  == expected_ultra_parameters["fixed_distance_boundaries_km"]
                  and metadata.get("within_fixed_bin_rank_strata")
                  == expected_ultra_parameters["within_fixed_bin_rank_strata"]
              ):
                  raise AssertionError(f"Ultra event metadata mismatch in {metadata_path}")
              return pd.read_csv(path), path

          minimum_finite = int(admission["minimum_finite_replicates"])
          maximum_cv = float(
              admission[
                  "maximum_replicate_coefficient_of_variation_at_guarded_material_cells"
              ]
          )
          rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
          for base_row in base.itertuples(index=False):
              key = (
                  str(base_row.family),
                  str(base_row.object_class),
                  str(base_row.source_cell_id),
                  str(base_row.record_id),
              )
              if key in ultra_keys:
                  table, path = ultra_table(*key)
                  column = f"log_q__{key[3]}"
                  tier = "event_hybrid_ultra_log"
                  representation = "log"
                  log_values = table[column].to_numpy(float)
              elif key in strong_keys:
                  table, path = strong_table(*key)
                  column = f"log_q__{key[3]}"
                  tier = "event_hybrid_strong_log"
                  representation = "log"
                  log_values = table[column].to_numpy(float)
              elif key in rank5_keys:
                  table, path = rank5_log_table(key[0], key[1], key[2])
                  column = f"log_q__{key[3]}"
                  tier = "rank5_log_replay"
                  representation = "log"
                  log_values = table[column].to_numpy(float)
              else:
                  prior = predecessor_by_key[key]
                  table, path = predecessor_table(str(prior.final_estimator_path))
                  column = f"q__{key[3]}"
                  values = table[column].to_numpy(float)
                  log_values = np.full(len(values), -np.inf, dtype=float)
                  np.log(values, out=log_values, where=values > 0.0)
                  tier = str(prior.final_estimator_tier)
                  representation = "linear_converted_to_log"
              result = log_replicate_quality(log_values, minimum_finite, maximum_cv)
              guarded_cell = (key[0], key[2]) in guarded
              finite_mean = math.isfinite(float(result["log_replicate_mean"]))
              passes = bool(finite_mean and (not guarded_cell or result["passes"]))
              failure_reasons: list[str] = []
              if not finite_mean:
                  failure_reasons.append("nonfinite_final_log_mean")
              if guarded_cell and not bool(result["passes"]):
                  failure_reasons.append("guarded_material_replicate_quality_failure")
              rows.append(
                  {
                      "family": key[0],
                      "object_class": key[1],
                      "source_cell_id": key[2],
                      "record_id": key[3],
                      "final_estimator_tier": tier,
                      "final_estimator_path": str(path.relative_to(repo)),
                      "final_probability_representation": representation,
                      "guarded_material_cell": guarded_cell,
                      **result,
                      "replicate_precision_passes": bool(result["passes"]),
                      "passes": passes,
                      "failure_reasons": ";".join(failure_reasons),
                  }
              )
          frame = pd.DataFrame(rows)
          final_quality_path = output / "rare_refinement_final_quality.csv"
          frame.to_csv(final_quality_path, index=False)
          failed = frame[~frame["passes"]]
          failed_examples = failed[
              [
                  "family",
                  "object_class",
                  "source_cell_id",
                  "record_id",
                  "final_estimator_tier",
                  "guarded_material_cell",
                  "log_replicate_mean",
                  "replicate_coefficient_of_variation",
                  "finite_log_replicates",
                  "failure_reasons",
              ]
          ].head(25).to_dict(orient="records")
          for item in failed_examples:
              for name in ("log_replicate_mean", "replicate_coefficient_of_variation"):
                  value = float(item[name])
                  if not math.isfinite(value):
                      item[name] = None
          audit = {
              "schema": "d0060-rare-refinement-final-audit-v4-log-domain-ultra",
              "log_domain_repair_frozen_before_any_log_replay_output": True,
              "ultra_refinement_rule_prospectively_frozen": True,
              "ultra_selection_is_location_blind": True,
              "ultra_replacement_is_independent_not_averaged": True,
              "ultra_failure_gate_not_relaxed": True,
              "event_probability_count": len(frame),
              "off_arc_event_probability_count": len(off_arc),
              "zero_offset_anchor_event_probability_count": len(zero),
              "minimum_finite_replicates": minimum_finite,
              "maximum_replicate_coefficient_of_variation_at_guarded_material_cells": maximum_cv,
              "guarded_material_event_probability_count": int(frame["guarded_material_cell"].sum()),
              "nonmaterial_event_probability_count": int((~frame["guarded_material_cell"]).sum()),
              "guarded_material_replicate_precision_failure_count": int(
                  (frame["guarded_material_cell"] & ~frame["replicate_precision_passes"]).sum()
              ),
              "nonmaterial_replicate_precision_failure_count": int(
                  (
                      ~frame["guarded_material_cell"]
                      & ~frame["replicate_precision_passes"]
                  ).sum()
              ),
              "strong_event_refinement_computed_count": len(strong),
              "strong_event_refinement_replaced_by_ultra_count": len(ultra_targets),
              "strong_event_refinement_retained_count": int(
                  frame["final_estimator_tier"].eq("event_hybrid_strong_log").sum()
              ),
              "ultra_event_refinement_count": int(
                  frame["final_estimator_tier"].eq("event_hybrid_ultra_log").sum()
              ),
              "estimator_tier_counts": {
                  str(key): int(value)
                  for key, value in frame["final_estimator_tier"].value_counts().items()
              },
              "passing_event_probability_count": int(frame["passes"].sum()),
              "failed_event_probability_count": len(failed),
              "unresolved_exact_zero_count": int(
                  (~np.isfinite(frame["log_replicate_mean"])).sum()
              ),
              "linear_probability_underflow_count": int(
                  frame["linear_mean_underflows"].sum()
              ),
              "all_final_log_probabilities_finite": bool(
                  np.isfinite(frame["log_replicate_mean"]).all()
              ),
              "all_guarded_material_replicate_quality_gates_pass": bool(
                  frame.loc[frame["guarded_material_cell"], "passes"].all()
              ),
              "nonmaterial_cv_is_diagnostic_not_global_admission_gate": True,
              "all_quality_gates_pass": bool(frame["passes"].all()),
              "alpha_or_pseudocount_used": False,
              "input_hashes": {
                  str(config_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(config_path),
                  str(repair_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(repair_path),
                  str(selector_audit_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(selector_audit_path),
                  str(strong_target_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(strong_target_path),
                  str(guarded_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(guarded_path),
                  str(predecessor_quality_archive.relative_to(repo)): sha256(
                      predecessor_quality_archive
                  ),
                  str(predecessor_audit_archive.relative_to(repo)): sha256(predecessor_audit_archive),
                  str(log_target_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(log_target_path),
                  str(strong_manifest_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(strong_manifest_path),
                  str(ultra_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(ultra_path),
                  str(ultra_selector_audit_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(ultra_selector_audit_path),
                  str(strong_quality_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(strong_quality_path),
                  str(ultra_target_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(ultra_target_path),
                  str(ultra_manifest_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(ultra_manifest_path),
                  str(final_quality_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(final_quality_path),
              },
              "failed_examples": failed_examples,
          }
          audit_path = output / "rare_refinement_final_audit.json"
          audit_path.write_text(json.dumps(audit, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
          print(json.dumps(audit, indent=2))
          if not audit["all_quality_gates_pass"]:
              raise SystemExit(2)


      if __name__ == "__main__":
          main()

      #!/usr/bin/env python3
      """Apply predeclared numerical gates to final D-0060 surfaces.

      The debris and isotope branches receive separate admission decisions. A failed
      isotope gate does not invalidate the debris-only likelihood. All displayed
      normalizations use the declared uniform strip-coordinate reference; downstream
      flight-prior integration must instead consume the raw family log likelihoods.
      """

      from __future__ import annotations

      import json
      import math
      from pathlib import Path

      import numpy as np
      import pandas as pd

      from build_ordinary_surfaces import FAMILIES, array_from_cells, cell_geometry
      from file_integrity import sha256
      from surface_core import (
          hellinger_distance,
          bayesian_model_average_log_likelihood,
          mode_index,
          normalize_log_surface,
          total_variation,
      )


      ISOTOPE_VARIANTS = (
          "debris_plus_isotope_central",
          "debris_plus_isotope_short_memory_tight",
          "debris_plus_isotope_long_memory_wide",
      )


      def add_gate(
          rows: list[dict[str, object]],
          gate: str,
          scope: str,
          passes: bool,
          value: object,
          threshold: object,
      ) -> None:
          rows.append(
              {
                  "gate": gate,
                  "scope": scope,
                  "passes": bool(passes),
                  "value": value,
                  "threshold": threshold,
              }
          )


      def weighted_event_log_contributions(
          events: pd.DataFrame,
      ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, int]:
          log_probability = events[
              "final_log_detection_reporting_probability_raw"
          ].to_numpy(float)
          invalid = (
              np.isnan(log_probability)
              | np.isposinf(log_probability)
              | (log_probability > 1e-12)
          )
          if invalid.any():
              raise AssertionError("Invalid final event log probability")
          contribution = events["report_allocated_weight_base"].to_numpy(float) * log_probability
          return contribution, int(np.count_nonzero(~np.isfinite(log_probability)))

      def debris_surfaces(
          analysis: Path,
          event_path: Path,
          offsets: list[float],
          family_prior: np.ndarray,
      ) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], np.ndarray, int]:
          events = pd.read_csv(event_path)
          contribution, nonfinite_count = weighted_event_log_contributions(events)
          events["weighted_log_likelihood_contribution"] = contribution
          grouped = (
              events.groupby(["family", "source_cell_id"], as_index=False)[
                  "weighted_log_likelihood_contribution"
              ]
              .sum()
              .rename(
                  columns={"weighted_log_likelihood_contribution": "raw_log_likelihood"}
              )
          )
          grid, measure = cell_geometry(analysis, offsets)
          grouped = grouped.merge(
              grid[["source_cell_id", "arc_bin_index", "cross_arc_offset_nm"]],
              on="source_cell_id",
              how="left",
              validate="many_to_one",
          )
          result: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
          raw_family: list[np.ndarray] = []
          for family in FAMILIES:
              selected = grouped[grouped["family"] == family]
              raw = array_from_cells(selected, "raw_log_likelihood", offsets)
              mass, _ = normalize_log_surface(raw, measure)
              result[f"{family}_raw"] = raw
              result[f"{family}_mass"] = mass
              raw_family.append(raw)
          raw_bma = bayesian_model_average_log_likelihood(
              np.stack(raw_family, axis=0), family_prior
          )
          mass_bma, _ = normalize_log_surface(raw_bma, measure)
          result["bma_raw"] = raw_bma
          result["bma_mass"] = mass_bma
          return result, measure, nonfinite_count


      def mode_shift(left: np.ndarray, right: np.ndarray) -> tuple[int, int]:
          left_mode = mode_index(left)
          right_mode = mode_index(right)
          return (
              abs(left_mode[0] - right_mode[0]),
              abs(left_mode[1] - right_mode[1]),
          )


      def ordered_array(
          frame: pd.DataFrame,
          variant: str,
          column: str,
          offsets: list[float],
          family: str | None = None,
      ) -> np.ndarray:
          selected = frame[frame["likelihood_variant"] == variant]
          if family is not None:
              selected = selected[selected["family"] == family]
          return array_from_cells(selected, column, offsets)


      def surface_frame_is_numerically_valid(
          frame: pd.DataFrame, mass_column: str
      ) -> bool:
          if frame.empty:
              return False
          raw = frame["raw_log_likelihood"].to_numpy(float)
          mass = frame[mass_column].to_numpy(float)
          return bool(
              not np.isnan(raw).any()
              and not np.isposinf(raw).any()
              and np.isfinite(mass).all()
              and np.all((mass >= 0.0) & (mass <= 1.0))
          )


      def boundary_axes_pass(
          along_endpoint_mass: float, cross_boundary_mass: float, limit: float
      ) -> bool:
          """Apply the frozen along- and cross-axis expansion rules separately."""
          return bool(
              along_endpoint_mass <= limit and cross_boundary_mass <= limit
          )


      def main() -> None:
          analysis = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
          output = analysis / "outputs" / "production"
          config = json.loads((analysis / "inputs" / "config.json").read_text())
          gate_config = config["admission_gates"]
          offsets = [float(value) for value in config["production_offsets_nm"]]
          expected_cells = len(pd.read_csv(analysis / "inputs" / "source_grid.csv"))
          family_prior = np.asarray(
              [
                  float(config["reference_current_family_prior"][family])
                  for family in FAMILIES
              ]
          )
          nested_particles = int(config["nested_convergence_particles_per_cell"])
          nested_path = output / f"event_probabilities_final_{nested_particles}.csv"
          full_path = output / "event_probabilities_final.csv"
          nested, measure, nested_nonfinite = debris_surfaces(
              analysis, nested_path, offsets, family_prior
          )
          full, _, full_nonfinite = debris_surfaces(
              analysis, full_path, offsets, family_prior
          )

          gates: list[dict[str, object]] = []
          add_gate(
              gates,
              "final_event_log_probabilities_are_finite",
              "debris",
              nested_nonfinite == 0 and full_nonfinite == 0,
              f"nested_nonfinite={nested_nonfinite};full_nonfinite={full_nonfinite}",
              0,
          )

          rare_quality_path = output / "rare_refinement_final_audit.json"
          rare_quality = json.loads(rare_quality_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          if rare_quality.get("schema") != "d0060-rare-refinement-final-audit-v4-log-domain-ultra":
              raise AssertionError("Unexpected final rare-refinement audit schema")
          rare_quality_passes = bool(
              rare_quality["all_final_log_probabilities_finite"]
              and rare_quality["all_guarded_material_replicate_quality_gates_pass"]
              and rare_quality["all_quality_gates_pass"]
          )
          add_gate(
              gates,
              "rare_event_log_replicate_quality_at_guarded_material_cells",
              "debris",
              rare_quality_passes,
              (
                  f"failed={rare_quality['failed_event_probability_count']};"
                  f"unresolved_nonfinite_log={rare_quality['unresolved_exact_zero_count']};"
                  f"linear_underflows={rare_quality['linear_probability_underflow_count']}"
              ),
              (
                  f"finite_replicates>={rare_quality['minimum_finite_replicates']};"
                  "all_final_log_means_finite;"
                  "guarded_material_"
                  f"replicate_CV<={rare_quality['maximum_replicate_coefficient_of_variation_at_guarded_material_cells']}"
              ),
          )

          rare_convergence_path = output / "rare_log_surface_convergence_audit.json"
          rare_convergence = json.loads(
              rare_convergence_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
          )
          if rare_convergence.get("schema") != "d0060-rare-log-surface-convergence-v2-final-refinement":
              raise AssertionError("Unexpected rare-convergence audit schema")
          add_gate(
              gates,
              "rare_event_log_refinement_surface_convergence",
              "debris",
              bool(rare_convergence["all_gates_pass"]),
              (
                  f"first4_vs_all8={rare_convergence['first_half_vs_full_replay_passes']};"
                  f"replay_vs_final={rare_convergence['replay_vs_final_refinement_passes']};"
                  f"final_guard_covered={rare_convergence['final_guard_contained_in_provisional_guard']}"
              ),
              "all predeclared TV, mode-shift, and material-guard coverage gates pass",
          )

          convergence_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
          max_tv = float(
              gate_config["maximum_production_profile_total_variation_8192_to_16384"]
          )
          max_lat_shift = int(
              gate_config["maximum_production_mode_shift_latitude_bins"]
          )
          max_offset_shift = int(
              gate_config["maximum_production_mode_shift_offset_bins"]
          )
          for estimator in (*FAMILIES, "bma"):
              tv = total_variation(
                  nested[f"{estimator}_mass"], full[f"{estimator}_mass"]
              )
              lat_shift, offset_shift = mode_shift(
                  nested[f"{estimator}_raw"], full[f"{estimator}_raw"]
              )
              passes = (
                  tv <= max_tv
                  and lat_shift <= max_lat_shift
                  and offset_shift <= max_offset_shift
              )
              convergence_rows.append(
                  {
                      "estimator": estimator,
                      "nested_particles_per_cell": nested_particles,
                      "full_particles_per_cell": int(
                          config["production_particles_per_cell"]
                      ),
                      "total_variation_distance": tv,
                      "mode_shift_arc_bins": lat_shift,
                      "mode_shift_cross_arc_bins": offset_shift,
                      "passes": passes,
                      "weighted_replacements_held_fixed": True,
                  }
              )
              add_gate(
                  gates,
                  f"{estimator}_nested_ordinary_convergence",
                  "debris",
                  passes,
                  (
                      f"TV={tv:.6g};arc_shift={lat_shift};"
                      f"cross_shift={offset_shift}"
                  ),
                  (
                      f"TV<={max_tv:g};arc_shift<={max_lat_shift};"
                      f"cross_shift<={max_offset_shift}"
                  ),
              )

          for estimator in FAMILIES:
              for cross_index, offset_nm in enumerate(offsets):
                  try:
                      nested_mass, _ = normalize_log_surface(
                          nested[f"{estimator}_raw"][:, cross_index],
                          measure[:, cross_index],
                      )
                      full_mass, _ = normalize_log_surface(
                          full[f"{estimator}_raw"][:, cross_index],
                          measure[:, cross_index],
                      )
                  except ValueError:
                      tv = math.inf
                      lat_shift = 25
                      profile_has_support = False
                      passes = False
                  else:
                      tv = total_variation(nested_mass, full_mass)
                      nested_mode = int(
                          np.argmax(nested[f"{estimator}_raw"][:, cross_index])
                      )
                      full_mode = int(
                          np.argmax(full[f"{estimator}_raw"][:, cross_index])
                      )
                      lat_shift = abs(nested_mode - full_mode)
                      profile_has_support = True
                      passes = tv <= max_tv and lat_shift <= max_lat_shift
                  convergence_rows.append(
                      {
                          "estimator": estimator,
                          "cross_arc_offset_nm": offset_nm,
                          "nested_particles_per_cell": nested_particles,
                          "full_particles_per_cell": int(
                              config["production_particles_per_cell"]
                          ),
                          "total_variation_distance": tv,
                          "mode_shift_arc_bins": lat_shift,
                          "mode_shift_cross_arc_bins": 0,
                          "passes": passes,
                          "profile_has_finite_support": profile_has_support,
                          "weighted_replacements_held_fixed": True,
                      }
                  )
                  add_gate(
                      gates,
                      f"{estimator}_offset_{offset_nm:+g}_nested_profile_convergence",
                      "debris",
                      passes,
                      f"TV={tv:.6g};arc_shift={lat_shift}",
                      f"TV<={max_tv:g};arc_shift<={max_lat_shift}",
                  )

          family = pd.read_csv(output / "final_family_raw_likelihood_surfaces.csv")
          combined = pd.read_csv(output / "final_equal_prior_bma_surfaces.csv")
          debris_family = family[family["likelihood_variant"] == "debris_only"]
          debris_combined = combined[combined["likelihood_variant"] == "debris_only"]
          debris_complete = len(debris_family) == len(FAMILIES) * expected_cells and len(
              debris_combined
          ) == expected_cells
          debris_finite = bool(
              np.isfinite(debris_family["raw_log_likelihood"]).any()
              and np.isfinite(debris_combined["raw_log_likelihood"]).any()
          )
          debris_numerically_valid = surface_frame_is_numerically_valid(
              debris_family, "uniform_strip_reference_conditional_cell_mass"
          ) and surface_frame_is_numerically_valid(
              debris_combined, "uniform_strip_reference_cell_mass"
          )
          add_gate(
              gates,
              "complete_debris_raw_family_and_reference_bma_lattices",
              "debris",
              debris_complete and debris_finite and debris_numerically_valid,
              (
                  f"family_rows={len(debris_family)};combined_rows={len(debris_combined)};"
                  f"numerically_valid={debris_numerically_valid}"
              ),
              f"family_rows={len(FAMILIES) * expected_cells};combined_rows={expected_cells};no_nan_or_posinf",
          )
          isotope_family = family[family["likelihood_variant"].isin(ISOTOPE_VARIANTS)]
          isotope_combined = combined[
              combined["likelihood_variant"].isin(ISOTOPE_VARIANTS)
          ]
          isotope_complete = len(isotope_family) == len(FAMILIES) * expected_cells * len(
              ISOTOPE_VARIANTS
          ) and len(isotope_combined) == expected_cells * len(ISOTOPE_VARIANTS)
          isotope_finite = bool(
              np.isfinite(isotope_family["raw_log_likelihood"]).any()
              and np.isfinite(isotope_combined["raw_log_likelihood"]).any()
          )
          isotope_numerically_valid = surface_frame_is_numerically_valid(
              isotope_family, "uniform_strip_reference_conditional_cell_mass"
          ) and surface_frame_is_numerically_valid(
              isotope_combined, "uniform_strip_reference_cell_mass"
          )
          add_gate(
              gates,
              "complete_isotope_raw_family_and_reference_bma_lattices",
              "isotope",
              isotope_complete and isotope_finite and isotope_numerically_valid,
              (
                  f"family_rows={len(isotope_family)};combined_rows={len(isotope_combined)};"
                  f"numerically_valid={isotope_numerically_valid}"
              ),
              (
                  f"family_rows={len(FAMILIES) * expected_cells * len(ISOTOPE_VARIANTS)};"
                  f"combined_rows={expected_cells * len(ISOTOPE_VARIANTS)};no_nan_or_posinf"
              ),
          )

          final = pd.read_csv(final_path)
          final_surface, final_rows = surface_arrays(
              analysis,
              final,
              "final_log_detection_reporting_probability_raw",
              offsets,
              family_prior,
          )
          rows = comparison_rows(
              "rank5_log_first4_vs_all8",
              half_surface,
              full_surface,
              maximum_tv,
              maximum_arc_shift,
              maximum_cross_shift,
          )
          rows.extend(
              comparison_rows(
                  "log_replay_vs_final_refinement",
                  full_surface,
                  final_surface,
                  maximum_tv,

          convergence = pd.DataFrame(rows)
          convergence_path = output / "rare_log_surface_convergence.csv"
          convergence.to_csv(convergence_path, index=False)

          guard = float(
              repair["material_refinement"]["guarded_relative_likelihood_threshold"]
          )
          top_count = int(repair["material_refinement"]["minimum_top_cells_per_estimator"])
          threshold = math.log(guard)
          required_guard: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
          for estimator, selected in final_rows.groupby("family"):
              material = selected[selected["relative_log_likelihood"] >= threshold]
              top = selected.nlargest(top_count, "raw_log_likelihood")
              if estimator == "bma":
                  for source_cell_id in pd.concat([material, top])["source_cell_id"].astype(str):
                      for family in FAMILIES:
                          required_guard.add((family, source_cell_id))
              else:
                  for source_cell_id in pd.concat([material, top])["source_cell_id"].astype(str):
                      required_guard.add((str(estimator), source_cell_id))
          guarded_frame = pd.read_csv(guarded_path)
          observed_guard = set(
              zip(
                  guarded_frame["family"].astype(str),
                  guarded_frame["source_cell_id"].astype(str),
              )
          )
          missing_guard = sorted(required_guard - observed_guard)
          guard_rows = pd.DataFrame(
              [
                  {
                      "family": family,
                      "source_cell_id": source_cell_id,
                      "present_in_provisional_guard": (family, source_cell_id) in observed_guard,
                  }
                  for family, source_cell_id in sorted(required_guard)
              ]
          )
          guard_audit_path = output / "rare_final_guard_coverage.csv"
          guard_rows.to_csv(guard_audit_path, index=False)
          audit = {
              "schema": "d0060-rare-log-surface-convergence-v2-final-refinement",
              "first_half_vs_full_replay_passes": bool(
                  convergence.loc[
                      convergence["comparison"].eq("rank5_log_first4_vs_all8"),
                      "passes",
                  ].all()
              ),
              "replay_vs_final_refinement_passes": bool(
                  convergence.loc[
                      convergence["comparison"].eq(
                          "log_replay_vs_final_refinement"
                      ),
                      "passes",
                  ].all()
              ),
              "final_guard_contained_in_provisional_guard": not missing_guard,
              "missing_final_guard_family_cells": [
                  {"family": family, "source_cell_id": source_cell_id}
                  for family, source_cell_id in missing_guard
              ],
              "all_gates_pass": bool(convergence["passes"].all() and not missing_guard),
              "maximum_total_variation": maximum_tv,
              "maximum_mode_shift_arc_bins": maximum_arc_shift,
              "maximum_mode_shift_cross_arc_bins": maximum_cross_shift,
              "guarded_relative_likelihood_threshold": guard,
              "minimum_top_cells_per_estimator": top_count,
              "alpha_or_pseudocount_used": False,
              "input_hashes": {
                  str(config_path.relative_to(analysis.parents[1])): sha256(config_path),
                  str(repair_path.relative_to(analysis.parents[1])): sha256(repair_path),
                  str(provisional_path.relative_to(analysis.parents[1])): sha256(provisional_path),
                  str(log_quality_path.relative_to(analysis.parents[1])): sha256(log_quality_path),
                  str(final_path.relative_to(analysis.parents[1])): sha256(final_path),
                  str(guarded_path.relative_to(analysis.parents[1])): sha256(guarded_path),
              },
              "output_hashes": {
                  str(convergence_path.relative_to(analysis.parents[1])): sha256(convergence_path),
                  str(guard_audit_path.relative_to(analysis.parents[1])): sha256(guard_audit_path),
              },
          }
          audit_path = output / "rare_log_surface_convergence_audit.json"
          audit_path.write_text(json.dumps(audit, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
          print(json.dumps(audit, indent=2))
          if not audit["all_gates_pass"]:
              raise SystemExit(2)


      if __name__ == "__main__":
          main()
