      The 8,192→16,384 ordinary comparison holds weighted replacements fixed and is not
      a full propagation of cross-target Monte Carlo covariance. Event-level precision
      diagnostics and surface convergence bound the tested effect, but the residual
      covariance limitation remains explicit in the downstream export.

      ## Barnacle isotope update

      The public A2-G1 record supplies 49 nonblank sample rows from only the final months
      before recovery. It cannot directly observe the March 2014 origin. D-0060 evaluates
      the same NOAA OISST analysis and local estimated error along both current-family
      path ensembles, marginalizes the supplied 17 January, 25 February, and 5 April
      2015 shell-age maps with equal prior probability, and separates shared bias,
      temporally correlated path discrepancy, measurement error, and OISST error.
      These record, chronology, and gridded-analysis facts are respectively grounded at
      `[S-al-qattan-et-al-2023-barnacle-isotopes, p. 2, Introduction; author-PDF
      lines 134–144]`, `[S-herbert-2023-mh370-barnacle-dataset-v4,
      Isotope_day2.csv, rows 2–50; Isotope_day_interpolate.csv]`, and
      `[S-noaa-oisst-v2-highres-2015, NOAA OPeNDAP sst and err attributes]`.

      The central covariance model is accompanied by mandatory short-memory/tight and
      long-memory/wide sensitivities. Material cells must use the final 4,096-particle ×
      8-replicate isotope-aware weighted estimator. Both particle-weight and root-lineage
      median per-replicate arrival ESS must be at least 50, and both corresponding
      isotope-weighted ESS values at least 20. Root-lineage ESS first aggregates cloned
      descendants by their original trajectory. Runtime checks require exactly the same
      likelihood totals as the particle calculation and no larger ESS; a pooled ESS is
      not substituted for these per-replicate diagnostics.

      If flaperon arrival probability is exactly zero, the conditional isotope mark is
      undefined but the joint remains exactly zero. An undefined isotope mark on positive
      debris support is fatal. No path-resolved seawater-δ18O field or oldest/largest
      flaperon barnacle record is available; those remain material limitations.
      The source authors identify shell-age assignment as the most sensitive assumption
      and recommend evaluating earlier and later starts
      `[S-al-qattan-et-al-2023-barnacle-isotopes, p. 8, section 3.4; author-PDF
      lines 440–450]`.

      Pinpointed source passages and dataset hashes are recorded in
      `knowledge/citations/D-0060-cross-arc-isotope-ledger.md`.

      ## Other barnacle evidence considered

      `[SOURCE]` A 2024 *Lepas anatifera* study exposed specimens to extreme
      temperature and salinity treatments for only 72 hours; its temperature table
      reports 80% survival at 7–10°C, 100% at the 27°C control, and 50% at 32°C
      `[S-ivan-baragano-et-al-2024-lepas-ecological-bounds, p. 2, methods;
      pp. 3–4, Table 2; repository PDF visually inspected, immutable local hash
      unavailable]`. Those acute, non-flaperon percentages do not calibrate a
      15-month path-survival hazard. D-0060 therefore does not turn them into another
      likelihood term; doing so would impose an unvalidated, overconfident ecological
      filter.

      The isotope paper also discusses Mg/Ca and proposes radiocarbon or amino-acid
      channels, but no admitted measurement for those channels is available
      `[S-al-qattan-et-al-2023-barnacle-isotopes, p. 9, section 3.4; author-PDF
      lines 486–490]`. Colonization timing is represented only through the three
      supplied shell-age maps. Component-specific colonization, growth, mortality,
      seawater-δ18O, or older-barnacle data could support future likelihood factors if
      their acquisition and calibration were available; they are not invented here.

      The maps normalize each family-conditional surface, and a separate reference

      | Analytical precision/calibration | `[same, p. 4, sections 2.3–2.4; author-PDF lines 223–250]` | `[SOURCE]` | Reports isotope precision and regression method. D-0060 keeps calibration/measurement uncertainty distinct from path discrepancy. |
      | Central 154-day age map | `[same, pp. 4–5, section 2.5; author-PDF lines 253–274]` | `[SOURCE]` | Reports recovery anchoring, approximate age, and 25 February colonization date. This is one chronology, not certain timing. |
      | Published current/SST experiment | `[same, p. 5, section 2.5; author-PDF lines 275–287]` | `[SOURCE]` model output | 50,000 particles used HYCOM reanalysis at 1/12° and 3-hour resolution. D-0060 does not reuse these particles. |
      | Published DTW ranking | `[same, p. 5, section 2.6; author-PDF lines 288–298]` | `[SOURCE]` method | DTW radius was three days; only the five best tracks were inspected; no sensitivity analysis was performed. A DTW rank is not a normalized likelihood. |
      | Réunion arrival among best tracks | `[same, p. 7, section 3.2; author-PDF lines 379–390]` | `[SOURCE]` model result | One of five highlighted tracks reached within 220 km. This is not a source probability and already uses arrival information. |
      | Oldest barnacles unavailable | `[same, p. 8, section 3.4; author-PDF lines 433–439]` | `[SOURCE]` | Only a partial reconstruction is public; older samples could be materially more informative. |
      | Age model sensitivity | `[same, p. 8, section 3.4; author-PDF lines 440–450]` | `[SOURCE]` | Authors identify age assignment as most sensitive and recommend earlier/later starts. D-0060 marginalizes all three supplied maps. |
      | Resolution/inter-specimen limits | `[same, pp. 8–9, section 3.4; author-PDF lines 451–467]` | `[SOURCE]` | Sampling resolution falls after day 57 and profiles differ slightly. D-0060 uses 49 sample rows, not daily interpolation as independent data. |
      | Wave/wind omission | `[same, p. 9, section 3.4; author-PDF lines 468–478]` | `[SOURCE]` | Authors recommend Stokes/wind additions. D-0060 paths include object-class-specific Stokes and leeway; only the complete flaperon uses field-test-centred calibration, while other classes use declared priors. |
      | Mg/Ca and proposed alternatives | `[same, p. 9, section 3.4; author-PDF lines 486–490]` | `[SOURCE]` | Mg/Ca is not admitted; proposed 14C/amino-acid channels lack measurements for this run. |
      | Dataset identity | `[same, p. 10, Data Availability; author-PDF lines 513–517]`; DOI `10.17632/nr4vt4h8gt.4` | `[SOURCE]` | Links the paper to the version-4 numerical dataset acquired and hash-checked locally. |
      | Forty-nine sample rows | `[S-herbert-2023-mh370-barnacle-dataset-v4, Isotope_day2.csv, 49 nonblank data rows; SHA-256 553916...]` | `[EXTRACTED]` | Admitted isotope observations. Blank padding is ignored. |
      | Three chronology maps | `[same dataset, Isotope_day_interpolate.csv; SHA-256 bc9806...]` | `[EXTRACTED]` | Early, central, and late shell-age mappings receive equal prior probability. |
      | OISST path mark | `[S-noaa-oisst-v2-highres-2015, NOAA OPeNDAP sst and err attributes; local NetCDF hashes]` | `[SOURCE]` gridded analysis | Shared SST/error field is applied to both current families. It is not fully acquisition-independent of assimilated observations in HYCOM. |
      | Acute ecological tolerance | `[S-ivan-baragano-et-al-2024-lepas-ecological-bounds, p. 2, methods; pp. 3–4, Table 2; repository PDF visually inspected, immutable local hash unavailable]` | `[SOURCE]` | The 72-hour treatment is not a calibrated long-duration survival model or a flaperon observation, so it is not converted to a trajectory likelihood. |

      ## Admission decision

      - `[DERIVED]` D-0060 may multiply the debris likelihood by
        \(p(I\mid R_{\mathrm{flaperon}},s,n,m)\) once.
      - `[DERIVED]` It must not multiply by both the conditional isotope density and
        another flaperon-arrival likelihood; the debris term already contains that
        recovery.
      - `[ASSUMPTION]` The central state-space covariance and the equal chronology
        prior are declared model choices. Tight/short-memory and wide/long-memory
        variants are mandatory sensitivities.
      - `[UNKNOWN]` Until production convergence and rare-event recovery conditioning
        pass, the isotope channel is not yet admitted to the integrated estimator.
      - `[DERIVED]` Every material selected-cell estimate must pass both particle-weight
        and root-lineage ESS gates; BMA-material cells require both family estimators.
      - `[ASSUMPTION]` The `0.32 °C` per-sample observation term is adopted from
        the reported analytical-precision scale; it is not a direct A2-G1
        repeated-measurement variance estimate.
      - `[UNKNOWN]` No path-resolved seawater-δ18O observation field is available.
        The shared-bias and correlated-discrepancy states bound plausible effects but
        do not independently reconstruct seawater isotope history.

                f"- Ordinary ensemble: {computation['ordinary_particles_per_cell']:,} particles per cell.",
                f"- Rare-event trajectory groups: {computation['rare_trajectory_groups']} at "
                f"{computation['rare_particles_per_replicate']:,} × "
                f"{computation['rare_replicates']} replicates.",
                f"- Inherited zero-offset weighted anchors: {computation['zero_offset_anchor_event_rows']} "
                f"event rows in {computation['zero_offset_anchor_trajectory_groups']} "
                "trajectory groups, subject to the same objective refinement rule.",
                f"- Objective linear-screen refinements: rank10={computation['rare_refinement_rank10_groups']} "
                f"groups at {computation['rare_refinement_rank10_particles_per_replicate']:,} × "
                f"{computation['rare_refinement_rank10_replicates']}; "
                f"rank5={computation['rare_refinement_rank5_groups']} groups at "
                f"{computation['rare_refinement_rank5_particles_per_replicate']:,} × {computation['rare_refinement_rank5_replicates']}.",
                f"- Matched log-domain replay: {computation['rare_refinement_rank5_log_replay_groups']} groups at "
                f"{computation['rare_refinement_rank5_log_particles_per_replicate']:,} × "
                f"{computation['rare_refinement_rank5_log_replicates']} replicates, using the same paths as rank5.",
                f"- Strong event-specific hybrid refinement: {computation['rare_refinement_strong_event_rows']} event rows at "
                f"{computation['rare_refinement_strong_particles_per_replicate']:,} × "
                f"{computation['rare_refinement_strong_replicates']} replicates; "
                f"{computation['rare_refinement_strong_retained_event_rows']} retained in the final estimator.",
                f"- Ultra event-specific replacement: {computation['rare_refinement_ultra_event_rows']} objectively selected rows independently rerun at "
                f"{computation['rare_refinement_ultra_particles_per_replicate']:,} × {computation['rare_refinement_ultra_replicates']} replicates; "
                f"{computation['rare_refinement_ultra_replacement_event_rows']} estimates replace their triggering strong estimates without averaging.",
                f"- Isotope screen cells: {computation['isotope_screen_cells']}; "
                f"final cells: {computation['isotope_final_cells']}.",
                f"- Final nonfinite canonical log-probability rows: "
                f"{computation['final_nonfinite_log_probability_count']}.",
                f"- Finite probabilities below the binary64 linear range: "
                f"{computation['final_linear_probability_underflow_count']} rows.",
                f"- Nonmaterial rare rows outside the guarded precision limit: "
                f"{computation['rare_refinement_nonmaterial_precision_failure_rows']} (exported for downstream re-guarding).",
                "- Alpha or pseudocount used: **no**. Underflowed linear diagnostics remain exact finite natural logs in the estimator; no floor is substituted.",
                "",
            ]
        )
        return "\n".join(lines)


    def main() -> None:
        family = pd.read_csv(OUTPUT / "final_family_raw_likelihood_surfaces.csv")
        combined = pd.read_csv(OUTPUT / "final_equal_prior_bma_surfaces.csv")
        summaries: list[dict[str, object]] = []
        for variant in VARIANTS:
            bma = combined[combined["likelihood_variant"] == variant]
            summaries.append(
                summarize_surface(
                    "equal_prior_hycom_gdp_bayesian_model_average",
                    variant,
                    bma,
                    "uniform_strip_reference_cell_mass",
                )
            )
            for current_family in ("hycom", "gdp"):
                selected = family[
                    (family["family"] == current_family)
                    & (family["likelihood_variant"] == variant)
                ]
                summaries.append(
                    summarize_surface(
                        f"{current_family}_family_conditional",
                        variant,
                        selected,
                        "uniform_strip_reference_conditional_cell_mass",
                    )
                )
        modes = pd.DataFrame(summaries)
        modes.to_csv(OUTPUT / "final_result_surface_summary.csv", index=False)

        admission = load_json(OUTPUT / "final_admission_audit.json")
        if admission.get("schema") != "d0060-final-admission-audit-v5-log-domain-ultra":
            raise AssertionError("Unexpected final admission audit schema")
        gates = pd.read_csv(OUTPUT / "final_admission_gates.csv")
        evidence = pd.read_csv(OUTPUT / "final_family_evidence.csv")
        event_audit = load_json(OUTPUT / "final_event_audit.json")
        ordinary_matrix = load_json(OUTPUT / "matrix_run.json")
        rare_target = load_json(OUTPUT / "rare_target_audit.json")
        zero_anchor = load_json(OUTPUT / "zero_offset_anchor_audit.json")
        rare_matrix = load_json(OUTPUT / "rare_matrix_run.json")
        rank10_matrix = load_json(OUTPUT / "rare_refinement_rank10_matrix_run.json")
        rank5_matrix = load_json(OUTPUT / "rare_refinement_rank5_matrix_run.json")
        rank5_log_matrix = load_json(OUTPUT / "rare_refinement_rank5_log_matrix_run.json")
        strong_log_matrix = load_json(OUTPUT / "rare_refinement_event_hybrid_strong_matrix_run.json")
        ultra_log_matrix = load_json(OUTPUT / "rare_refinement_event_hybrid_ultra_matrix_run.json")
        rare_log_convergence = load_json(OUTPUT / "rare_log_surface_convergence_audit.json")
        rare_quality = load_json(OUTPUT / "rare_refinement_final_audit.json")
        screen_matrix = load_json(OUTPUT / "isotope_screen_matrix_run.json")
        final_matrix = load_json(OUTPUT / "isotope_weighted_matrix_run.json")
        integration = load_json(OUTPUT / "integration_export_metadata.json")
        final_events = pd.read_csv(OUTPUT / "event_probabilities_final.csv")
        inventory = pd.read_csv(
            ANALYSIS.parent
            / "D-0043-generative-debris-drift"
            / "inputs"
            / "recovery_observations.csv"
        )
        debris_evidence = summarize_debris_evidence(final_events, inventory)
        grid_summary = summarize_source_grid(
            pd.read_csv(ANALYSIS / "inputs" / "source_grid.csv")
        )
        generalized_bayes_sensitivity = build_generalized_bayes_sensitivity(ANALYSIS)
        northern_branch = optional_manifest("final_northern_branch_summary.json")


        family_probabilities: list[dict[str, object]] = []
        for row in evidence.itertuples(index=False):
            family_probabilities.append(
                {
                    "likelihood_variant": str(row.likelihood_variant),
                    "family": str(row.family),
                    "prior_probability": float(row.family_prior_probability),
                    "generalized_family_weight_under_uniform_strip_reference": float(
                        row.generalized_family_weight_under_uniform_strip_reference
                    ),
                    "uniform_strip_reference_log_evidence": float(
                        row.uniform_strip_reference_log_evidence
                    ),
                }
            )

        prefix_audits = event_audit["nested_prefixes"]
        if not isinstance(prefix_audits, list):
            raise AssertionError("Final-event prefix audit is malformed")
        full_prefix = max(
            prefix_audits,
            key=lambda item: int(item["particles_per_cell"]),
        )
        payload = {
            "schema": "d0060-final-result-summary-v5-ultra-provenance",
            "epistemic_status": (
                "conditional likelihood result; not an end-of-flight posterior"
            ),
            "admission": admission,
            "debris_evidence": debris_evidence,
            "gate_counts": {
                "total": len(gates),
                "passed": int(gates["passes"].sum()),
                "failed": int((~gates["passes"]).sum()),
                "failed_by_scope": {
                    str(scope): int((~selected["passes"]).sum())
                    for scope, selected in gates.groupby("scope")
                },
            },
            "surface_summary_file": "final_result_surface_summary.csv",
            "surface_summaries": [
                {key: scalar(value) for key, value in row.items()}
                for row in summaries
            ],
            "generalized_family_weights_under_uniform_strip_reference": family_probabilities,
            "generalized_bayes_learning_rate_sensitivity": generalized_bayes_sensitivity,
            "northern_branch_diagnostic": northern_branch,
            "computation": {
                **grid_summary,
                "ordinary_total_tasks": int(ordinary_matrix["total_task_count"]),
                "ordinary_particles_per_cell": int(
                    ordinary_matrix["particles_per_cell"]
                ),
                "ordinary_maximum_parallel_processes": int(
                    ordinary_matrix["maximum_parallel_processes"]
                ),
                "rare_selected_event_rows": int(
                    rare_target["selected_event_probability_count"]
                ),
                "zero_offset_anchor_event_rows": int(
                    zero_anchor["weighted_anchor_event_probability_count"]
                ),
                "zero_offset_anchor_trajectory_groups": int(
                    zero_anchor["weighted_anchor_trajectory_group_count"]
                ),
                "rare_trajectory_groups": int(rare_matrix["total_task_count"]),
                "rare_particles_per_replicate": int(
                    rare_matrix["particles_per_replicate"]
                ),
                "rare_replicates": int(rare_matrix["replicates"]),
                "rare_refinement_rank10_groups": int(rank10_matrix["total_task_count"]),
                "rare_refinement_rank10_particles_per_replicate": int(
                    rank10_matrix["particles_per_replicate"]
                ),
                "rare_refinement_rank10_replicates": int(rank10_matrix["replicates"]),
                "rare_refinement_rank5_groups": int(rank5_matrix["total_task_count"]),
                "rare_refinement_rank5_particles_per_replicate": int(
                    rank5_matrix["particles_per_replicate"]
                ),
                "rare_refinement_rank5_replicates": int(rank5_matrix["replicates"]),
                "rare_refinement_rank5_log_replay_groups": int(
                    rank5_log_matrix["total_task_count"]
                ),
                "rare_refinement_rank5_log_particles_per_replicate": int(
                    rank5_log_matrix["particles_per_replicate"]
                ),
                "rare_refinement_rank5_log_replicates": int(rank5_log_matrix["replicates"]),
                "rare_refinement_strong_event_rows": int(
                    strong_log_matrix["total_task_count"]
                ),
                "rare_refinement_strong_particles_per_replicate": int(
                    strong_log_matrix["particles_per_replicate"]
                ),
                "rare_refinement_strong_replicates": int(strong_log_matrix["replicates"]),
                "rare_refinement_strong_retained_event_rows": int(rare_quality["strong_event_refinement_retained_count"]),
                "rare_refinement_ultra_event_rows": int(ultra_log_matrix["total_task_count"]),
                "rare_refinement_ultra_particles_per_replicate": int(ultra_log_matrix["particles_per_replicate"]),
                "rare_refinement_ultra_replicates": int(ultra_log_matrix["replicates"]),

                "rare_refinement_final_nonfinite_log_rows": int(

                ),

                "isotope_screen_cells": int(screen_matrix["total_task_count"]),
                "isotope_screen_particles_per_replicate": int(

        path = OUTPUT / "final_result_summary.json"
        path.write_text(
            json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"

        (OUTPUT / "final_result_summary.md").write_text(

        )
        print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))

      import json
      import math
      import sys
      from pathlib import Path

      import pandas as pd

      ANALYSIS = Path("/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/MH370-legacy-pre-refactor-20260824/analyses/D-0060-cross-arc-debris-drift")
      sys.path.insert(0, str(ANALYSIS / "src"))

      from log_rare_refinement_core import log_replicate_quality
      from run_strong_log_rare_event import event_stem

      targets = pd.read_csv(ANALYSIS / "inputs/rare_event_hybrid_strong_target_matrix.csv")
      root = ANALYSIS / "outputs/production/rare_refinement_event_hybrid_strong"
      done = 0
      passing = 0
      nonfinite = 0
      failures = {}
      for row in targets.itertuples(index=False):
          stem = event_stem(
              str(row.family),
              str(row.object_class),
              str(row.source_cell_id),
              str(row.record_id),
          )
          path = root / f"{stem}.csv"
          if not path.is_file():
              continue
          quality = log_replicate_quality(
              pd.read_csv(path)[f"log_q__{row.record_id}"].to_numpy(float), 4, 2.0
          )
          done += 1
          passing += int(quality["passes"])
          nonfinite += int(not math.isfinite(float(quality["log_replicate_mean"])))
          if not quality["passes"]:
              reason = str(quality["failure_reasons"])
              failures[reason] = failures.get(reason, 0) + 1

      print(
          json.dumps(
              {
                  "complete": done,
                  "passing": passing,
                  "ultra_triggering": done - passing,
                  "nonfinite_log_means": nonfinite,
                  "failure_breakdown": failures,
              },
              indent=2,
          )
      )

    ── Created /tmp/d0060_provisional_ultra_quality.py +46
      import json
      import math
      import sys
      from pathlib import Path

      import pandas as pd

      ANALYSIS = Path("/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/MH370-legacy-pre-refactor-20260824/analyses/D-0060-cross-arc-debris-drift")
      sys.path.insert(0, str(ANALYSIS / "src"))

      from log_rare_refinement_core import log_replicate_quality
      from run_ultra_log_rare_event import ultra_event_stem

      targets = pd.read_csv(ANALYSIS / "inputs/rare_event_hybrid_ultra_target_matrix.csv")
      root = ANALYSIS / "outputs/production/rare_refinement_event_hybrid_ultra"
      done = 0
      passing = 0
      nonfinite = 0
      failures = {}
      for row in targets.itertuples(index=False):
          stem = ultra_event_stem(
              str(row.family),
              str(row.object_class),
              str(row.source_cell_id),
              str(row.record_id),
          )
          path = root / f"{stem}.csv"
          if not path.is_file():
              continue
          quality = log_replicate_quality(
              pd.read_csv(path)[f"log_q__{row.record_id}"].to_numpy(float), 4, 2.0
          )
          done += 1
          passing += int(quality["passes"])
          nonfinite += int(not math.isfinite(float(quality["log_replicate_mean"])))
          if not quality["passes"]:
              reason = str(quality["failure_reasons"])
              failures[reason] = failures.get(reason, 0) + 1

      print(
          json.dumps(
              {
                  "complete": done,
                  "passing": passing,
                  "failing_final_gate": done - passing,
                  "nonfinite_log_means": nonfinite,
                  "failure_breakdown": failures,
              },
              indent=2,
          )

      import sys
      from pathlib import Path

      import pandas as pd

      ANALYSIS = Path("/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/MH370-legacy-pre-refactor-20260824/analyses/D-0060-cross-arc-debris-drift")
      sys.path.insert(0, str(ANALYSIS / "src"))

      from log_rare_refinement_core import log_replicate_quality
      from run_ultra_log_rare_event import ultra_event_stem

      targets = pd.read_csv(ANALYSIS / "inputs/rare_event_hybrid_ultra_target_matrix.csv")
      root = ANALYSIS / "outputs/production/rare_refinement_event_hybrid_ultra"
      rows = []
      for row in targets.itertuples(index=False):
          stem = ultra_event_stem(
              str(row.family),
              str(row.object_class),
              str(row.source_cell_id),
              str(row.record_id),
          )
          path = root / f"{stem}.csv"
          if not path.is_file():
              continue
          quality = log_replicate_quality(
              pd.read_csv(path)[f"log_q__{row.record_id}"].to_numpy(float), 4, 2.0
          )
          rows.append(
              {
                  "task": stem,
                  "log_mean": quality["log_replicate_mean"],
                  "cv": quality["replicate_coefficient_of_variation"],
                  "finite_replicates": quality["finite_log_replicates"],
                  "passes": quality["passes"],
                  "failure_reasons": quality["failure_reasons"],
              }
          )

      print(pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("task").to_string(index=False))

      --- src/build_integration_export.py

       import numpy as np
       import pandas as pd
       
       
      +def attach_admission_diagnostics(
      +    diagnostic: pd.DataFrame,
      +    admitted_variants: set[str],
      +) -> pd.DataFrame:
      +    """Mark admitted rows without hiding failed diagnostics.
      +
      +    Guarded precision failures invalidate an admitted variant, but they are an
      +    expected and material diagnostic when the frozen admission audit admits no
      +    variant.  Negative guarded counts always indicate inconsistent inputs.
      +    """
      +    result = diagnostic.copy()
      +    precision_failures = result[
      +        "debris_rare_replicate_precision_failure_count"
      +    ].to_numpy(int)
      +    nonmaterial_failures = result[
      +        "debris_nonmaterial_rare_replicate_precision_failure_count"
      +    ].to_numpy(int)
      +    guarded_failures = precision_failures - nonmaterial_failures
      +    if np.any(guarded_failures < 0):
      +        raise AssertionError(
      +            "A nonmaterial rare-event failure count exceeds the total"
      +        )
      +    result["debris_guarded_rare_replicate_precision_failure_count"] = (
      +        guarded_failures
      +    )
      +    result["admitted_for_downstream_integration"] = result[
      +        "likelihood_variant"
      +    ].isin(admitted_variants)
      +    admitted_rows = result[
      +        "admitted_for_downstream_integration"
      +    ].to_numpy(bool)
      +    if np.any(guarded_failures[admitted_rows] != 0):
      +        raise AssertionError(
      +            "An admitted likelihood variant contains a guarded material cell "
      +            "that failed rare-event precision"
      +        )
      +    return result
      +
      +

      --- src/build_log_final_events.py
      +++ src/build_log_final_events.py
      @@ -25,0 +26,31 @@
      +def validate_quality_for_diagnostic_composition(
      +    quality_audit: dict[str, object],
      +    quality: pd.DataFrame,
      +) -> bool:
      +    """Return the precision-gate result after proving the surface is composable."""
      +    expected_schema = "d0060-rare-refinement-final-audit-v4-log-domain-ultra"
      +    if quality_audit.get("schema") != expected_schema:
      +        raise AssertionError("Unexpected final rare-quality audit schema")
      +    row_finite = bool(
      +        np.isfinite(quality["log_replicate_mean"].to_numpy(float)).all()
      +    )
      +    audited_finite = bool(
      +        quality_audit.get("all_final_log_probabilities_finite", False)
      +    )
      +    if row_finite is not audited_finite:
      +        raise AssertionError("Final rare-quality finite-log status is inconsistent")
      +    quality_passes = bool(quality["passes"].eq(True).all())
      +    audited_passes = bool(quality_audit.get("all_quality_gates_pass", False))
      +    if quality_passes is not audited_passes:
      +        raise AssertionError("Final rare-quality precision status is inconsistent")
      +    failed_count = int((~quality["passes"].eq(True)).sum())
      +    if failed_count != int(
      +        quality_audit.get("failed_event_probability_count", -1)
      +    ):
      +        raise AssertionError("Final rare-quality failed-row count is inconsistent")
      +    if not row_finite:
      +        raise AssertionError(
      +            "A diagnostic surface cannot be composed from nonfinite log means"
      +        )
      +    return quality_passes
      +

    from decimal import Decimal, getcontext
    from pathlib import Path
    getcontext().prec=80
    for path in sorted(Path("/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/MH370-legacy-pre-refactor-20260824/analyses/D-0060-cross-arc-debris-drift/outputs/production/rare_refinement_event_hybrid_ultra").glob("ultra_*.csv")):
        if path.name.endswith("_resampling.csv"):
            continue
        with path.open(newline="",encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            rows=list(csv.DictReader(handle))
        cols=[name for name in rows[0] if name.startswith("log_q__")]
        if len(rows)"'!=8 or len(cols)!=1:
            continue
        values=[Decimal(row[cols[0]]) for row in rows]
        maximum=max(values)
        scaled=[(value-maximum).exp() for value in values]
        mean=sum(scaled)/Decimal(len(scaled))
        variance=sum((value-mean)**2 for value in scaled)/Decimal(len(scaled)-1)
        cv=variance.sqrt()/mean

    +#!/usr/bin/env python3
    +"""Independently recompute final rare-event quality with Decimal arithmetic."""
    +
    +from __future__ import annotations
    +
    +import csv
    +import hashlib
    +import json
    +from decimal import Decimal, localcontext
    +from pathlib import Path
    +
    +
    +PRECISION_DIGITS = 80
    +ABSOLUTE_TOLERANCE = Decimal("1e-10")
    +
    +
    +def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    +    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    +    with path.open("rb") as handle:
    +        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
    +            digest.update(chunk)
    +    return digest.hexdigest()
    +
    +
    +def parse_bool(value: object) -> bool:
    +    text = str(value).strip().lower()
    +    if text == "true":
    +        return True
    +    if text == "false":
    +        return False
    +    raise ValueError(f"Not a Boolean value: {value!r}")
    +
    +
    +def decimal_replicate_quality(
    +    serialized_values: list[str],
    +    *,
    +    representation: str,
    +    minimum_finite_replicates: int,
    +    maximum_coefficient_of_variation: Decimal,
    +    precision_digits: int = PRECISION_DIGITS,
    +) -> dict[str, object]:
    +    """Recompute the production gate without NumPy or binary exponentiation."""
    +    if len(serialized_values) < 2:
    +        raise ValueError("At least two replicates are required")
    +    if representation not in {"log", "linear_converted_to_log"}:
    +        raise ValueError(f"Unexpected probability representation: {representation}")
    +    with localcontext() as context:
    +        context.prec = precision_digits
    +        values = [Decimal(str(value)) for value in serialized_values]
    +        if representation == "log":
    +            if any(value.is_nan() or value == Decimal("Infinity") for value in values):
    +                raise ValueError("Log probabilities contain NaN or positive infinity")
    +            if any(value.is_finite() and value > 0 for value in values):
    +                raise ValueError("Log probabilities must lie in [-inf, 0]")
    +            finite = [value for value in values if value.is_finite()]
    +            finite_count = len(finite)
    +            if finite_count:
    +                maximum = max(finite)
    +                scaled = [
    +                    (value - maximum).exp()
    +                    if value.is_finite()
    +                    else Decimal(0)
    +                    for value in values
    +                ]
    +                mean = sum(scaled) / Decimal(len(scaled))
    +                log_mean = maximum + mean.ln()
    +            else:
    +                scaled = [Decimal(0) for _ in values]
    +                mean = Decimal(0)
    +                log_mean = Decimal("-Infinity")
    +        else:
    +            if any(not value.is_finite() for value in values):
    +                raise ValueError("Linear probabilities must be finite")
    +            if any(value < 0 or value > 1 for value in values):
    +                raise ValueError("Linear probabilities must lie in [0, 1]")
    +            finite_count = sum(value > 0 for value in values)
    +            scaled = values
    +            mean = sum(values) / Decimal(len(values))
    +            log_mean = mean.ln() if mean > 0 else Decimal("-Infinity")
    +
    +        if mean > 0:
    +            variance = sum(
    +                (value - mean) * (value - mean) for value in scaled
    +            ) / Decimal(len(scaled) - 1)
    +            coefficient = variance.sqrt() / mean
    +        else:
    +            coefficient = Decimal("Infinity")
    +        passes = bool(
    +            finite_count >= minimum_finite_replicates
    +            and coefficient <= maximum_coefficient_of_variation
    +        )
    +        return {
    +            "log_replicate_mean": +log_mean,
    +            "replicate_coefficient_of_variation": +coefficient,
    +            "finite_log_replicates": finite_count,
    +            "passes": passes,
    +        }
    +
    +
    +def decimal_residual(left: Decimal, right: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    +    if left.is_finite() and right.is_finite():
    +        return abs(left - right)
    +    if left == right:
    +        return Decimal(0)
    +    return Decimal("Infinity")
    +
    +
    +def main() -> None:
    +    analysis = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    +    repo = analysis.parents[1]
    +    output = analysis / "outputs" / "production"
    +    quality_path = output / "rare_refinement_final_quality.csv"
    +    quality_audit_path = output / "rare_refinement_final_audit.json"
    +    quality_audit = json.loads(quality_audit_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    +    expected_schema = "d0060-rare-refinement-final-audit-v4-log-domain-ultra"
    +    if quality_audit.get("schema") != expected_schema:
    +        raise AssertionError("Unexpected final rare-quality audit schema")
    +
    +    minimum_finite = int(quality_audit["minimum_finite_replicates"])
    +    maximum_cv = Decimal(
    +        str(
    +            quality_audit[
    +                "maximum_replicate_coefficient_of_variation_at_guarded_material_cells"
    +            ]
    +        )
    +    )
    +    with quality_path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    +        quality_rows = list(csv.DictReader(handle))
    +    key_names = ("family", "object_class", "source_cell_id", "record_id")
    +    keys = [tuple(str(row[name]) for name in key_names) for row in quality_rows]
    +    if len(set(keys)) != len(keys):
    +        raise AssertionError("Final rare-quality key is not unique")
    +
    +    table_cache: dict[Path, list[dict[str, str]]] = {}
    +    estimator_hashes: dict[str, str] = {}
    +    output_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    +    mismatch_count = 0
    +    decimal_guarded_failures = 0
    +    decimal_overall_failures = 0
    +    decimal_nonfinite_means = 0
    +    maximum_log_residual = Decimal(0)
    +    maximum_cv_residual = Decimal(0)
    +
    +    for row in quality_rows:
    +        representation = str(row["final_probability_representation"])
    +        estimator_path = repo / str(row["final_estimator_path"])
    +        if estimator_path not in table_cache:
    +            with estimator_path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    +                table_cache[estimator_path] = list(csv.DictReader(handle))
    +            estimator_hashes[str(estimator_path.relative_to(repo))] = sha256(
    +                estimator_path
    +            )
    +        table = table_cache[estimator_path]
    +        record_id = str(row["record_id"])
    +        column = (
    +            f"log_q__{record_id}"
    +            if representation == "log"
    +            else f"q__{record_id}"
    +        )
    +        if not table or column not in table[0]:
    +            raise AssertionError(f"Estimator table lacks {column}: {estimator_path}")
    +        decimal_quality = decimal_replicate_quality(
    +            [item[column] for item in table],
    +            representation=representation,
    +            minimum_finite_replicates=minimum_finite,
    +            maximum_coefficient_of_variation=maximum_cv,
    +        )
    +        production_log = Decimal(str(row["log_replicate_mean"]))
    +        production_cv = Decimal(
    +            str(row["replicate_coefficient_of_variation"])
    +        )
    +        log_residual = decimal_residual(
    +            production_log,
    +            decimal_quality["log_replicate_mean"],
    +        )
    +        cv_residual = decimal_residual(
    +            production_cv,
    +            decimal_quality["replicate_coefficient_of_variation"],
    +        )
    +        maximum_log_residual = max(maximum_log_residual, log_residual)
    +        maximum_cv_residual = max(maximum_cv_residual, cv_residual)
    +        finite_matches = int(row["finite_log_replicates"]) == int(
    +            decimal_quality["finite_log_replicates"]
    +        )
    +        precision_matches = parse_bool(
    +            row["replicate_precision_passes"]
    +        ) is bool(decimal_quality["passes"])
    +        guarded = parse_bool(row["guarded_material_cell"])
    +        decimal_finite = bool(
    +            decimal_quality["log_replicate_mean"].is_finite()
    +        )
    +        expected_overall_pass = bool(
    +            decimal_finite and (not guarded or decimal_quality["passes"])
    +        )
    +        overall_matches = parse_bool(row["passes"]) is expected_overall_pass
    +        row_matches = bool(
    +            log_residual <= ABSOLUTE_TOLERANCE
    +            and cv_residual <= ABSOLUTE_TOLERANCE
    +            and finite_matches
    +            and precision_matches
    +            and overall_matches
    +        )
    +        mismatch_count += int(not row_matches)
    +        decimal_guarded_failures += int(
    +            guarded and not bool(decimal_quality["passes"])
    +        )
    +        decimal_overall_failures += int(not expected_overall_pass)
    +        decimal_nonfinite_means += int(not decimal_finite)
    +        output_rows.append(
    +            {
    +                **{name: row[name] for name in key_names},
    +                "final_estimator_tier": row["final_estimator_tier"],
    +                "final_estimator_path": row["final_estimator_path"],
    +                "final_probability_representation": representation,
    +                "guarded_material_cell": guarded,
    +                "decimal_log_replicate_mean": str(
    +                    decimal_quality["log_replicate_mean"]
    +                ),
    +                "production_log_replicate_mean": row["log_replicate_mean"],
    +                "absolute_log_mean_residual": str(log_residual),
    +                "decimal_replicate_coefficient_of_variation": str(
    +                    decimal_quality["replicate_coefficient_of_variation"]
    +                ),
    +                "production_replicate_coefficient_of_variation": row[
    +                    "replicate_coefficient_of_variation"
    +                ],
    +                "absolute_cv_residual": str(cv_residual),
    +                "decimal_finite_log_replicates": decimal_quality[
    +                    "finite_log_replicates"
    +                ],
    +                "production_finite_log_replicates": row[
    +                    "finite_log_replicates"
    +                ],
    +                "decimal_replicate_precision_passes": decimal_quality["passes"],
    +                "production_replicate_precision_passes": parse_bool(
    +                    row["replicate_precision_passes"]
    +                ),
    +                "decimal_overall_passes": expected_overall_pass,
    +                "production_overall_passes": parse_bool(row["passes"]),
    +                "matches": row_matches,
    +            }
    +        )
    +
    +    csv_path = output / "rare_quality_decimal_independent.csv"
    +    if not output_rows:
    +        raise AssertionError("No final rare-quality rows were available")
    +    with csv_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    +        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=list(output_rows[0]))
    +        writer.writeheader()
    +        writer.writerows(output_rows)
    +
    +    expected_guarded_failures = int(
    +        quality_audit["guarded_material_replicate_precision_failure_count"]
    +    )
    +    expected_overall_failures = int(
    +        quality_audit["failed_event_probability_count"]
    +    )
    +    expected_nonfinite = int(quality_audit["unresolved_exact_zero_count"])
    +    audit_consistent = bool(
    +        decimal_guarded_failures == expected_guarded_failures
    +        and decimal_overall_failures == expected_overall_failures
    +        and decimal_nonfinite_means == expected_nonfinite
    +        and bool(quality_audit["all_final_log_probabilities_finite"])
    +        is (decimal_nonfinite_means == 0)
    +        and bool(
    +            quality_audit[
    +                "all_guarded_material_replicate_quality_gates_pass"
    +            ]
    +        )
    +        is (decimal_guarded_failures == 0)
    +        and bool(quality_audit["all_quality_gates_pass"])
    +        is (decimal_overall_failures == 0)
    +    )
    +    passes = bool(mismatch_count == 0 and audit_consistent)
    +    audit = {
    +        "schema": "d0060-independent-decimal-rare-quality-audit-v1",
    +        "passes": passes,
    +        "decimal_precision_digits": PRECISION_DIGITS,
    +        "absolute_comparison_tolerance": str(ABSOLUTE_TOLERANCE),
    +        "quality_row_count": len(output_rows),
    +        "unique_estimator_file_count": len(estimator_hashes),
    +        "mismatch_count": mismatch_count,
    +        "maximum_absolute_log_mean_residual": str(maximum_log_residual),
    +        "maximum_absolute_cv_residual": str(maximum_cv_residual),
    +        "decimal_guarded_material_precision_failure_count": (
    +            decimal_guarded_failures
    +        ),
    +        "quality_audit_guarded_material_precision_failure_count": (
    +            expected_guarded_failures
    +        ),
    +        "decimal_overall_failure_count": decimal_overall_failures,
    +        "quality_audit_overall_failure_count": expected_overall_failures,
    +        "decimal_nonfinite_log_mean_count": decimal_nonfinite_means,
    +        "quality_audit_nonfinite_log_mean_count": expected_nonfinite,
    +        "scientific_gate_failure_is_not_an_audit_mismatch": True,
    +        "input_hashes": {
    +            str(quality_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(quality_path),
    +            str(quality_audit_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(
    +                quality_audit_path
    +            ),
    +            **estimator_hashes,
    +        },
    +        "output_hashes": {
    +            str(csv_path.relative_to(repo)): sha256(csv_path),
    +        },
    +    }
    +    audit_path = output / "rare_quality_decimal_independent_audit.json"
    +    audit_path.write_text(
    +        json.dumps(audit, indent=2) + "\n",
    +        encoding="utf-8",
    +    )
    +    print(json.dumps(audit, indent=2))
    +    if not passes:
    +        raise SystemExit(2)
    +
    +
    +if __name__ == "__main__":
    +    main()

    --- test/test_log_rare_refinement.py
    +++ test/test_log_rare_refinement.py
    @@ -169,0 +170,47 @@
    +    def test_decimal_quality_independently_reproduces_cv_and_scale(self):
    +        keyword = {
    +            "minimum_finite_replicates": 1,
    +            "maximum_coefficient_of_variation": Decimal("3"),
    +        }
    +        log_result = decimal_replicate_quality(
    +            ["0"] + ["-Infinity"] * 7,
    +            representation="log",
    +            **keyword,
    +        )
    +        linear_result = decimal_replicate_quality(
    +            ["1"] + ["0"] * 7,
    +            representation="linear_converted_to_log",
    +            **keyword,
    +        )
    +        shifted_result = decimal_replicate_quality(
    +            ["-1000"] + ["-Infinity"] * 7,
    +            representation="log",
    +            **keyword,
    +        )
    +        expected_cv = math.sqrt(8.0)
    +        expected_log_mean = -math.log(8.0)
    +        for result in (log_result, linear_result):
    +            self.assertEqual(result["finite_log_replicates"], 1)
    +            self.assertTrue(result["passes"])
    +            self.assertAlmostEqual(
    +                float(result["replicate_coefficient_of_variation"]),
    +                expected_cv,
    +                delta=1e-14,
    +            )
    +            self.assertAlmostEqual(
    +                float(result["log_replicate_mean"]),
    +                expected_log_mean,
    +                delta=1e-14,
    +            )
    +        self.assertAlmostEqual(
    +            float(shifted_result["log_replicate_mean"]),
    +            -1000.0 + expected_log_mean,
    +            delta=1e-12,
    +        )
    +        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
    +            decimal_replicate_quality(
    +                ["0.1", "0"],
    +                representation="log",
    +                **keyword,
    +            )
    +

  ── Assistant
  I found one orchestration edge case before it could become a failure: the already-running process cached the old stage list, so it would later call the strengthened final audit without first creating the new Decimal artifacts. I’m installing a one-shot handoff guard for this cycle only; it will wait for the canonical rare-quality table, run the independent audit once, verify its outputs, and then exit.     ── Created /tmp/d0060_decimal_cached_cycle_guard.py +119
      #!/usr/bin/env python3
      """One-shot guard for the pre-Decimal cached D-0060 pipeline cycle."""

      from __future__ import annotations

      import hashlib
      import json
      import subprocess
      import sys
      import time
      from datetime import datetime, timezone
      from pathlib import Path


      ANALYSIS = Path(
          "/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/MH370-legacy-pre-refactor-20260824/"
          "analyses/D-0060-cross-arc-debris-drift"
      )
      OUTPUTS = ANALYSIS / "outputs"
      PRODUCTION = OUTPUTS / "production"
      PIPELINE_STATE = OUTPUTS / "pipeline_state.json"
      GUARD_STATE = OUTPUTS / "decimal_cached_cycle_guard_state.json"
      GUARD_LOG = OUTPUTS / "decimal_cached_cycle_guard.log"
      PIPELINE_PID = 1174608


      def utc_now() -> str:
          return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


      def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
          digest = hashlib.sha256()
          with path.open("rb") as handle:
              for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
                  digest.update(chunk)
          return digest.hexdigest()


      def command_line(pid: int) -> str:
          try:
              payload = (Path("/proc") / str(pid) / "cmdline").read_bytes()
          except (FileNotFoundError, PermissionError, ProcessLookupError):
              return ""
          return payload.replace(b"\0", b" ").decode("utf-8", errors="replace")


      def write_state(payload: dict[str, object]) -> None:
          temporary = GUARD_STATE.with_suffix(".tmp")
          temporary.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
          temporary.replace(GUARD_STATE)


      def main() -> None:
          state: dict[str, object] = {
              "schema": "d0060-pre-decimal-cached-cycle-guard-v1",
              "started_utc": utc_now(),
              "pipeline_pid": PIPELINE_PID,
              "status": "waiting_for_canonical_rare_quality_audit",
          }
          if "run_completion_pipeline.py" not in command_line(PIPELINE_PID):
              raise SystemExit("Expected cached completion-pipeline PID is not active")
          write_state(state)

          while True:
              pipeline_alive = "run_completion_pipeline.py" in command_line(PIPELINE_PID)
              if PIPELINE_STATE.is_file():
                  pipeline = json.loads(PIPELINE_STATE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                  steps = pipeline.get("steps", [])
                  rare_done = any(
                      item.get("name") == "rare_log_final_audit"
                      and item.get("status") == "complete"
                      for item in steps
                      if isinstance(item, dict)
                  )
                  if rare_done:
                      break
              if not pipeline_alive:
                  state.update(
                      status="failed_pipeline_ended_before_quality_audit",
                      finished_utc=utc_now(),
                  )
                  write_state(state)
                  raise SystemExit(2)
              state["updated_utc"] = utc_now()
              write_state(state)
              time.sleep(2.0)

          quality_audit_path = PRODUCTION / "rare_refinement_final_audit.json"
          quality_path = PRODUCTION / "rare_refinement_final_quality.csv"
          quality_audit = json.loads(quality_audit_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          if quality_audit.get("schema") != (
              "d0060-rare-refinement-final-audit-v4-log-domain-ultra"
          ):
              raise AssertionError("Canonical rare-quality audit does not have v4 schema")
          state.update(
              status="running_independent_decimal_audit",
              canonical_quality_audit_sha256=sha256(quality_audit_path),
              canonical_quality_table_sha256=sha256(quality_path),
              updated_utc=utc_now(),
          )
          write_state(state)
          with GUARD_LOG.open("wb") as handle:
              result = subprocess.run(
                  [sys.executable, str(ANALYSIS / "src" / "audit_rare_quality_decimal.py")],
                  cwd=ANALYSIS.parents[1],
                  stdout=handle,
                  stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                  check=False,
              )
          state["return_code"] = result.returncode
          if result.returncode != 0:
              state.update(status="failed_decimal_audit", finished_utc=utc_now())
              write_state(state)
              raise SystemExit(result.returncode)

          decimal_audit_path = PRODUCTION / "rare_quality_decimal_independent_audit.json"
          decimal_table_path = PRODUCTION / "rare_quality_decimal_independent.csv"
          decimal_audit = json.loads(decimal_audit_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
          if decimal_audit.get("schema") != (
              "d0060-independent-decimal-rare-quality-audit-v1"
          ) or decimal_audit.get("passes") is not True:
              raise AssertionError("Independent Decimal audit did not pass")
          if decimal_audit["input_hashes"][str(quality_path.relative_to(ANALYSIS.parents[1]))] != sha256(quality_path):
              raise AssertionError("Decimal audit is not bound to the canonical quality table")
          if decimal_audit["input_hashes"][str(quality_audit_path.relative_to(ANALYSIS.parents[1]))] != sha256(quality_audit_path):
              raise AssertionError("Decimal audit is not bound to the canonical quality audit")
          state.update(
              status="complete",
              decimal_audit_sha256=sha256(decimal_audit_path),
              decimal_table_sha256=sha256(decimal_table_path),
              finished_utc=utc_now(),
          )
          write_state(state)
          print(json.dumps(state, indent=2), flush=True)


      if __name__ == "__main__":
          main()

    ── Created /tmp/d0060_chart_layout_preflight.py +80
      #!/usr/bin/env python3
      """Exercise every D-0060 publication layout with a synthetic full grid."""

      from __future__ import annotations

      import json
      import sys
      from pathlib import Path

      import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
      import numpy as np
      import pandas as pd


      ANALYSIS = Path(
          "/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/MH370-legacy-pre-refactor-20260824/"
          "analyses/D-0060-cross-arc-debris-drift"
      )
      sys.path.insert(0, str(ANALYSIS / "src"))
      import make_2d_charts as charts


      def main() -> None:
          grid = pd.read_csv(ANALYSIS / "inputs" / "source_grid.csv")
          grid["uniform_strip_coordinate_quadrature_km2"] = 1.0
          # A smooth positive fixture ensures contour construction is exercised.
          arc = grid["arc_latitude"].to_numpy(float)
          offset = grid["cross_arc_offset_nm"].to_numpy(float)
          density = np.exp(-0.5 * ((arc + 32.0) / 5.0) ** 2 - 0.5 * (offset / 70.0) ** 2)
          grid["fixture_mass"] = density / density.sum()
          note = (
              "[DERIVED, CONDITIONAL] Uniform strip-coordinate reference; no end-of-flight "
              "prior. Fill is clipped only for display at 10⁻⁴ of peak density. "
              "Admission: debris=FAIL; isotope=FAIL / sensitivity only."
          )
          all_comparators = pd.read_csv(
              ANALYSIS.parent / "D-0055-bayesian-debris-integration" / "inputs" /
              "published_comparators.csv"
          )
          comparators = all_comparators[
              ~all_comparators["comparator_id"].isin(charts.EXCLUDED_PUBLISHED_COMPARATOR_IDS)
          ].copy()
          comparators.loc[
              comparators["comparator_id"].eq("griffin_csiro_2017"), "label"
          ] = "Griffin/CSIRO 2017 (Pleiades-conditioned)"
          context_note = (
              "[DERIVED/SOURCE COMPARISON; debris admission=FAIL] Density is the "
              "conditional D-0060 debris model average. Published ranges and markers "
              "are author outputs with different evidence and estimands; they are not "
              "pooled likelihood terms."
          )
          figures = [
              charts.single_figure(
                  grid,
                  mass_column="fixture_mass",
                  title="Debris only: equal-prior HYCOM–GDP model combination (generalized BMA)",
                  note=note,
              ),
              charts.published_context_figure(grid, comparators, context_note),
              charts.paired_figure(
                  grid,
                  grid,
                  mass_column="fixture_mass",
                  left_title="HYCOM family conditional",
                  right_title="GDP family conditional",
                  suptitle="D-0060 debris only: current-family comparison",
                  note=note,
              ),
              charts.triplet_figure(
                  [grid, grid, grid],
                  mass_column="fixture_mass",
                  titles=[
                      "Short-memory / tight covariance",
                      "Central covariance",
                      "Long-memory / wide covariance",
                  ],
                  suptitle="D-0060 A2-G1 isotope covariance sensitivity",
                  note=note,
              ),
          ]
          charts.CHART_LAYOUT_ROWS.clear()
          for index, figure in enumerate(figures, start=1):
              charts.validate_layout(figure, Path(f"synthetic-{index}"))
              plt.close(figure)
          print(json.dumps(charts.CHART_LAYOUT_ROWS, indent=2))


      if __name__ == "__main__":
          main()
